- **The gate** (GENERAL properties only; anything else keeps today's path byte-for-byte): `is3D` (a real volume) + the resolved GRID target is WebGPU + `transferControlToOffscreen` + **not an agent model** (the agent sphere pass already owns a layered canvas; compositing voxels with spheres in one depth buffer is a follow-up) + **no glyphs** (a main-thread overlay). **Alpha blend is the ONLY remaining frame-mode-only visual** (the WGSL pass does not back-to-front sort) — enabling it pins UI-sync ON, which hides the voxel canvas and hands the frame back to gl3d **exactly as before L1**; switching it off releases free mode again. **Occupancy AO AND cast shadows now run free-mode** (Phases 1/2 below) so they are NOT attach/`want` terms.
- **Free-mode occupancy AO (Phase 1 — [HANDOFF_VOXEL_FREEMODE_LIGHTING.md](docs/HANDOFF_VOXEL_FREEMODE_LIGHTING.md)).** The cube draw VS recomputes the SAME 6-face-neighbour occupancy scan gl3d does CPU-side in `uploadColors` (`ao = cnt/6`, bound-guarded, reading `colorsIn` via the compaction shader's `filled()`), passes it as a `@interpolate(flat)` varying, and the FS folds it onto the ambient term exactly like gl3d (`ambient·(1 − aoStrength·vAO) + diffuse·ndl`). `aoStrength` is a new `VoxelView` field (`@192`) fed by `computeVoxelRenderView` as `light.ao ? light.aoStrength : 0` — **0 ⇒ the VS skips the scan and the FS folds no darkening ⇒ byte-behaviour-identical to no AO**. The `postGridCamera` dedup key carries `aoStrength` so a toggle/slider re-presents; `light.ao` was REMOVED from `updateGridUiSync`'s `want`. Verified in-browser on the Accretor (300³ WebGPU, dense dendrite, dist 0.28): AO ON darkens crevices in free mode identically to the gl3d frame mode (Inspect-armed flip is seamless), toggling AO ON→OFF causes **0 `setGridUiSync` flips** (1 camera re-present each), AO OFF returns to the flat baseline.
- **Free-mode cast shadows (Phase 2 — same handoff).** A **depth-only shadow-map pass** (`VOXEL_SHADOW_WGSL` — the same procedurally-generated compacted cubes rendered from the light's ortho POV into a 2048² `depth24plus` texture, `cullMode:'none'`, FS discards clipped cubes) runs BEFORE the display cube pass in the SAME `presentVoxels` encoder, only when `voxelShadowOn`. The draw FS PCF-samples it (`shadowFactor`, 3×3 taps over a `sampler_comparison` + `texture_depth_2d`, binding 3/4) and folds `sh` onto diffuse+specular exactly like gl3d's `SHADOW_GLSL`. **The light matrix is SHARED**: `computeLightMVP(W,H,D,lightDir)` is now EXPORTED from gl3d (its private method delegates to it, the Phase-C `sceneCameraMatrices`/`lightWorldDirFor` precedent) so the two renderers can't disagree on the caster projection; `computeVoxelRenderView` calls it + the scale-relative bias. **GL→WebGPU clip adaptation** (gl3d's ortho is GL-convention NDC z∈[−1,1]/y-up, WebGPU is z∈[0,1]/y-down): the shadow VS applies `p.z = (p.z + p.w)·½`, and `shadowFactor` samples `uv = ndc.xy·(½,−½)+½` (the −½ flips y) + `zRef = ndc.z·½+½`. New `VoxelView` fields `shadowStrength @196` / `shadowBias @200` / `lightMVP @208` (a mat4 pinned by the align-16 padding at @204; `VOXEL_VIEW_BYTES` 208→272; `uploadVoxelView` LITERAL-indexes the 16 `lightMVP` floats `f[52..67]` so the layout harness's 0-based-loop parser detects them; `verify-render-uniform-layouts.mjs` green). **0 strength ⇒ the FS short-circuits `shadowFactor` to 1.0 AND the depth pass is skipped ⇒ byte-behaviour-identical to no shadows.** `light.shadows` REMOVED from `updateGridUiSync`'s `want`. **WGSL gotcha the verification caught: `ref` is a RESERVED KEYWORD** — the draw shader failed to compile (→ `setupVoxelRender` returned false → silent frame-mode fallback), renamed to `zRef`; `setupVoxelRender`'s previously-silent `catch` now `console.error`s the failure (a genuine hardening — the silent catch is exactly what hid this). Verified on the Accretor (300³ WebGPU, dense structure): shadows ON casts inter-beam shadows in free mode matching gl3d frame mode (Inspect flip seamless), toggling ON→OFF = **0 `setGridUiSync` flips** (1 re-present each), OFF returns to the flat baseline. Render-only, ZERO compiler changes (`git diff --stat` = webgpuRuntime.ts + SimulatorView.tsx + gl3d.ts [the `computeLightMVP` export only]).
- **The readback policy** (the ONLY sanctioned way to drop per-frame CPU work): `gridUiSync` (worker) mirrors `gridUiSyncPostedRef` (main thread). **FREE** = the voxel canvas owns the display and nothing crosses the wire. **FRAME** = the worker reads colours back and ships them so gl3d renders the frame and its colour-id `pick()` FBO resolves. The driver wants FRAME when: paused, recording, an inspect popover pinned/sweeping, **a pointer GESTURE is in progress on the GL canvas (press→release), or the Inspect toggle is armed / Shift is held while hovering**, or shadows/AO/alpha blend are on — debounced OFF ~300 ms. **PASSIVE HOVER DELIBERATELY DOES NOT PIN** (the shipped L1 driver's bare `glPointerOverRef` term did, so simply resting the cursor on the canvas held the readback path and the user saw no speedup at all — a reported regression). Nothing about hovering needs CPU state: the brush cursor + the brush are pure `pickOnPlane` ray math and a paint re-presents through the worker's own mutation tail; the ONE thing that does is gl3d's colour-id `pick()` (3D inspect), which reads the CPU instance buffer only frame mode refreshes — hence the gesture / Inspect-armed / Shift-held terms (Shift+LMB's pick fires on the RELEASE, so pressing the modifier first gives the flip its frame). `glGestureActiveRef` is cleared in `onUp` (a WINDOW listener, so it always fires) **and** in the effect teardown, so an unmount mid-gesture can't latch the pin on. The AGENT driver's hover term is already this narrow (it ANDs an ARMED agent brush) and is intentionally untouched. **`gridDisplayOwnedByGpu()`** is the ONE predicate `finalizeStepWebGPU`'s `wantColors` and `sendColors` both consult — it simply extends the historical `rt.directRender` term, so every existing consumer behaves identically. **NO second staleness mechanism was added**: the CPU **attrs** mirror keeps its existing one-shot rule (`gpuOwnsAttrs` / `ensureCpuAttrsFresh`), which every reading/mutating message already honours because 2D direct render has exercised it for years; `requestColorsSnapshot` gained the voxel arm alongside `directRender`.
- **Frame mode needs a colours buffer IN HAND** (`gridFrameAwaitingColorsRef`): flipping UI-sync ON sets it, and only a `stepped` carrying colours clears it — so the flip can never render a colours snapshot from thousands of generations ago. The worker's `setGridUiSync` OFF→ON ships one colours frame immediately (a PAUSED sim posts no step; the Phase C no-blank-frame fix).
- **`voxelRenderOn()` is DERIVED from `webgpuRuntime.voxelRender`, never mirrored in a module flag** — the pipelines die with the runtime (`destroyWebGPURuntime` → `destroyVoxelRender`) and a rebuilt runtime starts `false`, so every teardown/rebuild resets it for free and the "a REBUILT runtime must reset its flags" trap cannot bite. `useWebGPUStatus` carries the live `voxelRender` flag, so the main thread detects a rebuilt runtime and re-attaches with no extra message (verified: Recompile → `voxelRender:false` → re-attach → `voxelRenderStatus:true`).
- **Messages**: `attachVoxelCanvas` / `setGridCamera` / `setGridUiSync` / `refreshGridDisplay` (+ the ack `voxelRenderStatus`), mirroring the agent four. The **present hook is the tail of `runColorPassWebGPU()`** — every colour refresh (step-batch tail, mutation tails, the `colorPass` message, reset) routes through it; the no-OM branch of `refreshColorsAfterInputWebGPU` presents too. **BUT the colour pass is USER-SUPPRESSIBLE, and the display must not be** (the shipped-L1 regression): the step handler guards it with `if (!msg.skipColorPass)`, and the main thread sets `skipColorPass: unlimitedGens` — so at **∞ G/F** nothing presented and the voxel canvas stayed frozen on the frame it was ATTACHED with (an accretion model's 60-cell seed inside a 300³ box = "the canvas turns black"; measured 13 200 gens / 0 presents). Fixed with `voxelDisplayLive()` (= `voxelRenderOn() && !gridUiSync`, deliberately NARROWER than `gridDisplayOwnedByGpu()`) → `else if (voxelDisplayLive()) runColorPassWebGPU({ skipReductions: true })`: refresh + present **once per BATCH** (never per generation) whenever the worker-presented canvas is the live display. `skipReductions` exists because that finalize passes `needColors:false`, which already gates the reductions READBACK off — dispatching them would be one more whole pass over the grid nobody reads. **The 2D direct-render path has the same present-skip but it is NOT actionable**: its OffscreenCanvas is a *placeholder* that reaches the screen only via the main thread's `drawImage` inside `draw()`, which ∞ G/F skips by design — frozen-while-∞ is the intended 2D behaviour (verified unchanged: 459 batches / 45 800 gens / 0 colours). **The voxel canvas is a real DOM canvas the BROWSER composites, so a worker-side present alone changes what the user sees** — that asymmetry is the whole reason for the separate predicate. Companion fix on the main thread: the ∞ G/F fast path skips `draw()` entirely, which is where the free/frame flip + canvas VISIBILITY are decided, so it now calls `draw()` at its 2 Hz counter cadence **when `voxelRenderActiveRef`** (free-mode `draw()` is cheap — the O(total) `uploadColors` rescan is skipped); every other model keeps the historical skip-drawing-for-throughput behaviour byte-for-byte. **Rule for any future GPU-presented display: every batch that ends must leave the presented canvas showing that batch — never hang the only display refresh off a pass a display optimisation can switch off.** The `__voxelReadback` DEV probe reports a `presents` counter so "did anything present?" is a one-line question.
- **Screenshot / recording in free mode**: `capture3dPixels()` composites the worker's voxel canvas UNDER gl3d's overlays-only `readPixels` output (a transferred canvas is still a valid `CanvasImageSource` — the same property the 2D direct-render blit relies on), so a screenshot taken mid-run is never "overlays over nothing". Recording additionally forces UI-sync ON (verified end-to-end through the real buttons).
- **Verified** (real GPU, in-browser; the pane reports hidden, so protocol + GPU-buffer readbacks, never composited pixels): Life3D 96³ + WebGPU **engages** (`voxelRenderStatus:true` is posted only after both WGSL modules compiled 0 errors and a present ran); free mode advances generations with **no `colors`** on `stepped`; **the compaction is EXACT** — the GPU instance count read out of the indirect draw args equals an independently CPU-computed visible-cell count from a colours snapshot (4432 = 4432 sparse; **12432 → 6600** on a solid 20³ block, i.e. exactly 18³ = 5832 buried cells culled), every sampled compacted index is a genuinely visible cell, `vertexCount === 36`; the **hostile staleness probe** passes (after 20 free-mode gens `getState` agrees with the colours snapshot cell-for-cell over 884,736 cells with 0 mismatches, `setInspectCells` reports the right value, and a `paintManual` lands and reads back); Shadows / Occlusion / Alpha blend each flip UI-sync ON through the real checkboxes and release it when unchecked; **fallbacks unchanged** — a 2D WebGPU model still direct-renders (`directRender:true`, no voxel attach), a 3D WASM model still ships colours, and a glyph-3D model and an agent-3D model never attach; 0 worker errors and 0 console errors across the session.
- **Post-ship regression fix (2026-07-24)** — the two defects above (present hooked to a suppressible pass; passive hover pinning frame mode) were both reported by the user on Accretor 300³/WebGPU and are fixed; full root-cause + evidence in [docs/HANDOFF_LATTICE_GPU_L1.md](docs/HANDOFF_LATTICE_GPU_L1.md) §9. Verified on that exact model at ∞ G/F: the voxel canvas is shown while playing, `stepped` still carries no `colors`, and the presented volume tracks the sim (113 823 @ gen 500 → 735 108 @ gen 857) instead of freezing at the seed; the stop event hands off to gl3d with an exactly-matching instance count (748 288).
- **Post-ship regression fix #2 (2026-07-24) — free mode never displayed ANYTHING, and the ONE uniform-layout rule that follows.** Reported on the shipped Accretor 300³ (WebGPU, G/F 1): the voxel canvas rendered nothing, ever. **Root cause: the `VoxelView` WGSL struct and `uploadVoxelView` are two independent declarations of one byte layout, and they drifted.** `vec3<f32>` has align 16 but **SIZE 12**, and WGSL's rule is `offset(m) = roundUp(align(m), offset(prev) + size(prev))` — so the `ambient : f32` following `clipFwd : vec3<f32>` landed at byte **124**, inside clipFwd's trailing pad, while the writer wrote the scalar block from **128**. Every scalar shifted one float: **`cubeScale` read the SPECULAR slot (0 by default) ⇒ `local * cubeScale` collapsed all 36 vertices of every cube onto the cell centre ⇒ zero-area triangles ⇒ zero rasterised pixels** (and `clipEnabled` read garbage, which is why opening the clip interval didn't help). **Fix: `@align(16) ambient` pins the block back to 128, making the existing writer exactly correct** (one token; both sides now carry `// @byte` annotations). **Why every probe said it was healthy — the trap to remember:** `bg` is a `vec4`, whose 16-byte alignment RE-SYNCS the layout, so `gridW`/`gridWH`/`total`/`cullBuried` were read correctly — and those are the ONLY members the COMPACTION shader touches. So the compaction produced a genuinely correct instance count, `vertexCount:36`, and a rising `presents` counter while the draw drew nothing: a uniform desync can corrupt an arbitrary MIDDLE SLICE of a struct while leaving both ends right. **Rules:** (1) never let a scalar follow a `vec3` in a host-shared struct without pinning it (`@align(16)`, or write the pad deliberately); (2) **a GPU-buffer probe is NOT evidence a render works — only pixels are** ("presenting" ≠ "displaying"). Guarded permanently by the COMPUTED [scripts/verify-render-uniform-layouts.mjs](scripts/verify-render-uniform-layouts.mjs) — it implements the WGSL layout rules (incl. `@align`/`@size`), parses each registered struct + its writer's index assignments, and asserts **no write lands in padding** and **no member byte is left unwritten** (name-independent, so `half` ↔ `halfX/Y/Z` needs no mapping; registered: `VoxelView`, `RenderView3D`, `RenderView`; negative-controlled against all three mutation shapes). **Any new WGSL uniform struct filled by a hand-written TypedArray writer MUST be added to its REGISTRY.** `verify-agent-render.mjs` gained a **B10** block pinning the voxel attach wiring (presented canvas IS the DOM-attached element; the ack force-posts the camera past the dedup key; `presentVoxels` re-acquires the swap-chain texture each frame). Screenshot-verified end to end; full write-up: [HANDOFF_LATTICE_GPU_L1.md](docs/HANDOFF_LATTICE_GPU_L1.md) §10.
- **Post-ship regression fix #3 (2026-07-24) — the UI-SYNC MIRROR INVARIANT (pause / 3D inspect / shadows / AO / alpha blend / recording all silently died after free mode).** Reported on the shipped Accretor 300³ (WebGPU grid): after the driver posts `setGridUiSync {on:false}` ONCE, it **never posts `{on:true}` again for the rest of the session** — Pause gave zero posts + zero colour frames, and Shift+LMB gave no inspect popover (gl3d's colour-id `pick()` resolves against a CPU instance buffer only frame mode refreshes) — while the voxel canvas kept rendering perfectly, so nothing looked broken. **Root cause: the worker's `gridUiSync` is a MODULE flag that SURVIVES a re-attach on the SAME worker** (`draw()` re-attaches on any display CSS-size change — panel splitter, window resize, transport bar), **but the `voxelRenderStatus` ack ended with `gridUiSyncPostedRef.current = true; // worker default is ON`** — true only for a BRAND-NEW worker. A re-attach in FREE mode therefore stranded the mirror at ON while the worker sat at OFF, and the driver's `if (!gridUiSyncPostedRef.current)` guard then suppressed **every** later ON post; because the stuck value is ON, no OFF transition could resync it either, so the moment `want` turned true inside the ~300 ms window the desync became **permanent**. The **AGENT** driver (`agentUiSyncPostedRef` — used by far more models) had the identical shape and the identical latent bug in its `agentRenderStatus` ack. **Fix: the worker ACKS its live flag** (`voxelRenderStatus`/`agentRenderStatus` carry `uiSync: gridUiSync` / `uiSync: agentUiSync`) **and each ack handler mirrors `msg.uiSync`** (absent ⇒ ON, so a pre-fix/failure ack degrades to the historical assumption) + clears any pending OFF debounce armed for the previous attach. **The `if (!posted)` guard is KEPT** — deleting it would post on every driver call (the driver runs per stepped message and per pointer event). **THE INVARIANT (documented on `gridUiSyncPostedRef`, binding on BOTH mirrors): the mirror may ONLY be assigned from a value the worker has actually been TOLD (a post in the same statement), a value the worker has ACKED, or the module default of a BRAND-NEW worker created in the same tick — NEVER from an assumption.** Verified negative-controlled at both levels: with the ack reverted, a deterministic re-attach-then-want-true sequence reproduces the exact report (no ON post, worker stuck `false`, no popover); with the fix, the same sequence posts ON and `Cell (152, 152) · State E` opens mid-play, and all four consequences pass (pause; 3D inspect; shadows/AO/alpha-blend each pin ON and release OFF; recording pins on Record and releases on Stop & Save) — with the a4805a6 camera behaviour unregressed (a 20-move orbit drag = 11 `setGridCamera`, **0** UI-sync flips, **0** colour frames). Pinned by the **B11** block in [scripts/verify-agent-render.mjs](scripts/verify-agent-render.mjs) (11 checks over both drivers; 10-mutation negative control, all caught). Full write-up: [HANDOFF_LATTICE_GPU_L1.md](docs/HANDOFF_LATTICE_GPU_L1.md) §11.
- **Known scope + follow-ups**: the composited image / lighting-parity eyeball needs a VISIBLE pane (deferred to an orchestrator/user spot-check), as does the finite-G/F play loop (rAF-paced ⇒ suspended in a hidden pane; unchanged code). **Occupancy AO (Phase 1) AND cast shadows (Phase 2) now run free-mode (done).** **Alpha blend stays frame-mode-only by decision** (Phase 3 of the free-mode-lighting handoff): an exact GPU back-to-front sort needs the visible-instance COUNT CPU-side (fighting L1's whole no-readback design), and WBOIT is an approximation that breaks the *recording forces frame-mode ⇒ the two must match* invariant — so `alpha3d` keeps its `want` term (gl3d handles translucency exactly as before L1). A 3D grid+agents composite is also still a follow-up.

### 3D viewport — feedback round 3 (RMB pan, clickable gizmo POV, brush-plane indicator, hover cursor)
- **RMB pans** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) pointer effect): RMB (`button === 2`) is otherwise unused in 3D, so a right-drag now pans (in addition to Shift+MMB / Shift+Alt+LMB). The existing `onCtx` `preventDefault` suppresses the context menu. The gizmo-click check is LMB-only, so RMB/MMB still orbit/pan.
- **Clickable corner gizmo → 6 POVs** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): the gizmo now draws all **6 ± axis stubs + endpoint dots** (`GIZMO_ENDS` table; `gl_PointSize` via a new `uPointSize` uniform on `LINE_VS`; shared `gizmoMatrix()`/`gizmoSizePx()`/`drawLines()`). `gizmoHitTest(cssX,cssY,cssW,cssH)` maps a click (CSS→device px, bottom-left gizmo viewport at `(10,10)+S`) to the nearest projected endpoint `{axis, sign}` (NDC threshold 0.55, facing-the-camera tie-break for edge-on endpoints), or null outside. `onDown` checks it FIRST (LMB only, before any drag/paint) and `setPov(axis,sign)` snaps the camera: x→yaw 0/π, y→yaw ±π/2 (pitch 0); z→pitch ±1.49 keeping yaw (±1.49 stays shy of the `cameraBasis` pole degeneracy at ±π/2). Snaps instantly (no tween).
- **Brush-plane bounds + grid** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `renderBrushPlane()`): when the Brush plane is enabled, the renderer draws a bright cyan bounded rectangle + an interior grid ON the slice (depth-tested, so voxels in front occlude it) at the plane's world coordinate — `z = hz - pos` (z-plane), `y = pos - hy` (y), `x = pos - hx` (x), matching `pickOnPlane`'s mapping exactly so the drawn plane is precisely where the brush paints. `draw()` feeds it via `r.setBrushPlane(enabled ? {axis,pos} : null)`.
- **Hover cube cursor** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): an amber wireframe cube (12 edges, depth-test OFF so it's always visible like a cursor) outlines the hovered cell on the brush plane. SimulatorView's `onMove` calls `updateHover()` (= `pickOnPlane` at the cursor) on idle moves AND during a brush drag, **redrawing only when the hovered cell CHANGES** (a full GL re-render per raw pointermove is wasteful on large volumes); `onLeave` clears it. (**Round 4 generalised this to the full brush FOOTPRINT** — `setHoverCells`/`renderHoverCells` — see "feedback round 4". **Perf round** added the colors-upload gate so a re-render no longer re-scans the volume — see "3D perf — feedback round 5".)
- **Verified** (runtime): all 6 gizmo endpoints hit-test in-region (null outside); the 4 POVs snap to the expected yaw/pitch (x+→0/0, y+→π/2/0, z±→±1.49 keeping yaw) via a synthetic gizmo click; `renderBrushPlane` (+~43k px) and `renderHoverCube` (+~430 px, also without the plane) both add rendered pixels; an RMB-drag moved the camera target. Build + tsc clean.

### 3D viewport — feedback round 4 (footprint cursor, 3D line, default transparent, shift-inspect, bg colour, depth resize, focus)
- **Default voxel colours are TRANSPARENT in 3D** ([sim.worker.ts](src/simulator/engine/sim.worker.ts) `writeDefaultColors`): when `depth > 1` the fallback fills alpha 0 (`colors.fill(0)`) so the voxel renderer instances ZERO cells — a model with no Output Mapping (or before its first colour pass) no longer builds a full opaque volume on every recompile that the sim would erase. 2D keeps the visible first-bool fallback.
- **Footprint cursor** (the round-3 single cube → the whole brush shape): renderer `setHoverCell`→**`setHoverCells(cells[])`** + `renderHoverCube`→**`renderHoverCells`** (a wireframe cube per affected cell). SimulatorView's `updateHover` computes the FOOTPRINT into `hoverCells3dRef` via `footprintFor(hit)` (the line preview when a Line anchor is staged, else `mapStampToPlane(hit, currentStampOffsets())`); `draw()` feeds `r.setHoverCells(...)`. Redraw-on-change is kept, but a staged Line always recomputes (the preview grows with the cursor).
- **`mapStampToPlane(center, offsets)`** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): the SINGLE source of truth that maps 2D stamp offsets onto the plane's free axes (+ torus wrap) — used by `paint3dRef`'s `stampAt` (refactored to call it), the hover footprint, and the line. So the highlighted cursor is exactly what gets painted.
- **3D Line tool** (was: clicking acted like a circle + immediate apply): `line3dAnchorRef` two-click staging in `onDown` (first plain-LMB stages the plane-cell anchor, no paint; second commits `paintLine3d` = `lineFootprint` (2D `lineStampCells` in the plane's free coords → `mapStampToPlane`)). RMB / Escape / brush-shape / brush-mapping / brush-plane change all clear the anchor. No drag-paint in Line mode.
- **Shift+LMB inspects** (was: Ctrl/Cmd, and plain Shift+LMB painted): inspect was `active='inspect'` on `e.button===0 && (shiftKey||ctrlKey||metaKey) && !alt`; the colour-id `pick()` fires on a no-move pointer-up. Plain LMB still brushes; Shift+Alt/MMB still pan. (**Round 6 narrows this**: Ctrl/Cmd+LMB now RESIZES the brush, so ONLY Shift+LMB inspects — see "feedback round 6".)
- **Canvas click restores keyboard focus** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) `onDown`): blurs a focused `INPUT/TEXTAREA/SELECT` before `preventDefault`, so the transport shortcuts (Enter/Space/Esc) work after clicking the 3D canvas (the canvas isn't focusable; without the blur the last-edited field kept focus and the shortcut handler bailed).
- **Background colour** (3D View panel): `bg3d {enabled,color}` → `bg3dRef [r,g,b,a]|null` → renderer `setBackgroundColor` (used in `render()`'s `gl.clearColor`). Default transparent (page shows through); a checkbox + colour input fills it opaquely.
- **Depth in the simulator resize** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): `simDepth` state + a Depth `NumberField` shown in the Grid Dimensions panel only when 3D. `initWorkerWithDimensions(w, h, dOverride?)` honours the override (bakes `gridDepth` + `dimension` into the compiler `dimsModel` so WebGPU's baked `total` + the 3D codec match), unifies `d3` across the pan/zoom + init-message paths, and `setSimDepth(d3)`; 2D (no override) is unchanged.
- **Verified** (runtime): canvas click blurs the focused input (activeElement INPUT→BODY); bg red applies + transparent restores + the Background control/colour input exist; the footprint cursor renders a radius-4 disc (38280 amber px); the Line tool stages on the first plane click (0 paints) and commits a 160-cell capsule on the second; Shift+LMB does not paint; the Depth field shows in 3D; Life3D loads 24³. tsc + build clean.

### 3D perf — feedback round 5 (camera-only redraws skip the full-volume upload)
- **The win:** `draw()`'s 3D branch used to call `r.uploadColors(colors3d, total)` on EVERY redraw — orbit, pan, wheel, auto-orbit (rAF), hover, clip/viz toggles — re-scanning all `total` cells for alpha>0 and re-uploading the compacted instance buffer, even when only the CAMERA moved and the colours were unchanged. At 100³ that's ~1e6 cells scanned + a GPU upload PER FRAME of orbiting.
- **The gate** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): the worker hands a FRESH colours buffer on every `stepped` (`colorsRef.current = msg.colors`; never mutated in place — verified), so buffer IDENTITY changes iff colours changed. `draw()` now uploads only when `colors3d !== lastUploadedColors3dRef.current` (then stamps the ref); camera-only frames reuse the existing GPU instance buffer and just re-render. `lastUploadedColors3dRef` is reset to `null` when the renderer is recreated (the `[is3D]` init effect) and when the colours buffer is cleared on reinit (`initWorkerWithDimensions`), so a fresh renderer / resize always re-uploads. 2D is untouched (the gate is inside the 3D branch).
- **Verified** (runtime): 6 camera-only redraws (yaw changes) → **0** `uploadColors` calls; one simulation step → **1**. tsc + build clean.
- **NOT low-hanging (noted, not done):** the JS/WASM `buildNeighborIndices` precomputed per-cell neighbour table is `total × nSz` Int32 (≈104 MB at 100³ Moore; scales multiplicatively in 3D) — the dominant 3D memory cost. Avoiding it means inline neighbour computation like the WebGPU path (which already uses the compact offset table per §2.1), a substantial engine change that could also slow per-step JS/WASM. Out of scope for a "low-hanging" pass. Minor leftovers (per-frame `new Float32Array` for the static gizmo/overlay line buffers; the alpha-blend `sortBackToFront` keys/order/sorted allocation) are small and were left as-is.

### 3D perf — occlusion round (backface cull + buried-cell cull, cells only)
Two render-layer occlusion cuts in [gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts), both **provably image-identical in the modes they run in** and auto-disabled (with an internal re-compaction) in the modes where interiors are legitimately visible. Zero compiler / worker / schema / 2D impact; agents (spheres/sprites/metaballs) are untouched.
- **Backface culling (voxel cube passes)**: each cube is a closed convex solid with consistent CCW-outward winding (verified per-face via cross products on `CUBE`), so its backfaces are never visible while it renders closed + opaque — `gl.CULL_FACE` halves rasterized cube fragments for free. Scoped strictly around the three cube instanced draws (display / pick / shadow-map) and disabled after each, so the sphere/sprite/line/metaball passes never see it. Gates: the pick + shadow passes cull iff **no clip** (a straddling cube's surviving fragment at a cut can be a backface); the DISPLAY pass culls when **`alphaBlend || !clip.enabled`** — under ALPHA BLEND culling is always ON and is a **CORRECTNESS requirement, not an optimization**: with `depthMask(false)` every triangle blends in BUFFER order, so an uncculled translucent cube composites its dark backfaces over/under its front faces in an order unrelated to depth — incoherent per-cube colour + doubled opacity (the reported "cubes look just weird" with alive alpha 100 on Life3D). Culled, a convex cube contributes only its front faces (which never overlap in screen space) and the per-cube back-to-front sort gives correct Option-A ordering — translucent volumes read as coherent glass voxels with depth-accumulating opacity. Kept ON under blend even when clipped (see-through anyway; the blend-order argument still applies).
- **Buried-cell culling (`uploadColors`)**: a cell with all **6 face-neighbours filled** is dropped from the instance buffer — a ray's first hit on a union of FLUSH opaque cubes always lands on a face whose adjacent cell is empty, so a fully-enclosed cell can never be the visible surface (a dense volume collapses to its shell; a solid 10³ block instances 488 not 1000). Eligibility (`buriedCullEligible`) = **cell gaps OFF** (`cubeScale ≥ 1` — with the 0.92 gapped look the crack planes between cubes expose interior side faces at grazing angles, so gaps-ON never culls) AND **no alpha blend** AND **no clip interval**. Boundary cells never reach cnt 6 (bounds guards), so the volume's outer faces always render; the render never wraps the lattice, so torus is irrelevant. The 6-neighbour scan is SHARED with occupancy AO (`needNbr = aoOn || cullBuried`); when neither runs, the upload path is byte-identical to the historical scan. **`instanceCount` is therefore ≤ the alive count when buried culling ran** (older "instanceCount = alive-cell count" notes assume gaps ON / ineligibility).
- **Eligibility flips re-compact INTERNALLY**: `uploadColors` stashes `lastColors`/`lastTotal` **by reference** (the worker hands a fresh buffer per step, never mutates in place — the same identity contract as SimulatorView's upload gate), and `setAlphaBlend`/`setClipPlane`/`setCellGaps` call `refreshVoxelsIfCullingChanged()` — a cheap bool compare per frame that re-runs `uploadColors` from the stash only when `buriedCullApplied !== buriedCullEligible()`. So toggling the clip open immediately restores the buried cells (the cut shows a full interior, not a hollow shell) with no SimulatorView round-trip. **`clearVoxels()`** (new) zeroes `instanceCount` AND drops the stash — SimulatorView's agents-only/no-colours branch calls it instead of writing `r.instanceCount = 0`, so a later eligibility flip can't resurrect a previous model's grid from stale colours; `refreshVoxelsIfCullingChanged` is additionally guarded on `instanceCount > 0` as defence-in-depth.
- **Verified** (runtime, real GL): solid-10³ synthetic — culled 488 vs full 1000 instances, and the **pick-FBO structural test is EXACT** (0 of 588,240 pixels differ in visible-surface cell id between the culled set with culling ON and the full set with culling OFF); display images differ only by 23–39 sub-pixel MSAA coverage samples at silhouette edges (max channel delta ≤15 — the context is `antialias:true`, backfaces contribute edge coverage samples; imperceptible, not structural). Life3D end-to-end: alpha-128 OM variant renders translucent under Alpha blend (26k pixels change vs opaque; full set restored on the blend flip), clip slab shows the interior, pick returns valid ids, shadows + AO render with culling active, 0 GL errors. Morphogenesis — 3D Tissue: agents+bonds render, voxels stay 0 through eligibility flips (the clearVoxels path). tsc + `npm run build` clean.
- **The agent alpha chain is VERIFIED end-to-end and needs no render change** (the "agents show no transparency" report traced to the gradient EDITORS dropping `a` — fixed with the Color Scale round-trip — plus the 3D toggle below, NOT to the agent pipelines): a behaviour-graph `setCellLooks` with `_port_a` reaches `s.colors` on every agent target (agentWasm writes the `a` byte; the agentWebgpu readback unpacks all 4 packed bytes), the 2D overlay draws discs with `rgba(...)` + sprites via `globalAlpha` (pixel-proven: alpha-100 green over a white backdrop composites to exactly (190,243,210), 76k pixels, zero opaque-green pixels — **2D agent translucency is ALWAYS on**, like the 2D grid), and the 3D sphere impostors carry alpha in the instance buffer (`d[o+7]`) + blend/sort under the toggle (pixel-proven on 3D Tissue at alpha 100). **3D translucency (cells AND agents) requires the "Alpha blend" checkbox** — off, everything visible renders opaque (alpha-0 stays hidden either way); the checkbox tooltip now says so. The linked AGENT view path is likewise pixel-proven (a bool view's `linkedColors.gradient` stop with `a:100` → 88–98k exact-composite pixels); **NB a BOOL linked palette lives in `linkedColors.gradient` (2 stops at positions 0/1), NOT `linkedColors.tag`** — a hand-built `tag` palette on a bool attr is silently ignored (the builder falls back to the default black→white gradient).
- **The declared-but-unwired-alpha trap has a BADGE** ([CaNode.tsx](src/modeler/vpl/CaNode.tsx) `configIssues`): setting a non-opaque alpha on a Color Scale / Categorical Color / Colour Constant palette grows the node's `a` OUTPUT port (Option A) — but nothing flows until that port is **wired into Set Cell Looks' `a` input**; declared-but-unconsumed alpha has NO effect anywhere, which reads as "transparency doesn't work". The amber badge now says exactly that when `<node>HasAlpha(config) && !connectedHandles.has('output_value_a')` (the connected-handles pub/sub carries output handles). Verified: badge shows on an unwired alpha palette, disappears when the `a` edge is added, and the wired model renders translucent.
- **METABALLS honour agent alpha** (both views; previously the fused blob was opaque no matter what — "regardless of metaballs" nothing changed). **3D = PER-VOXEL** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): a first version used one density-weighted MEAN alpha for the whole blob — user-rejected ("the whole body became transparent instead of just the colors where I put the transparency"). The bake now writes a **SECOND R8 3D texture** (`metaAlphaTex`, unit 3) holding `Σ(w·a)/Σw` per voxel; `META_FS` samples it at the raymarch hit point, so each blob REGION inherits its LOCAL agents' alpha — a translucent species reads translucent where it is, an opaque one stays opaque, and species boundaries fade trilinearly. Blend runs (depth-write off) only when `agentAlphaBlend && metaFieldHasAlpha` (any baked agent non-opaque); a 1×1 opaque R8 dummy keeps the sampler valid on the opaque path (`uAlphaOn=0`), and **R8 uploads need `UNPACK_ALIGNMENT 1`** (rows aren't 4-byte multiples for arbitrary field widths). Verified: synthetic two-cluster field — the translucent cluster's 806 px changed 100%, the opaque cluster's 904 px changed 0% on the blend toggle; the real palette→wire→bake chain on 3D Tissue works via the actual checkboxes; a stock all-opaque model's blob is untouched by the toggle (0 px, `fieldHasAlpha` false); 0 GL errors. **2D goo = MEAN (approximation)** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): the mean alpha rides a SECOND `feColorMatrix` **fade stage** appended to the goo filter (threshold saturates to [0,1] between primitives, then the fade scales alpha — exact cap at the mean) — per-region alpha is NOT feasible in the SVG-filter pipeline (the ratio Σw·a/Σw needs a divide; filters can't). **KNOWN LIMITATION (user-acknowledged, possible follow-up): the translucent blob is FIRST-HIT surface blending, not volumetric** — the march stops at the first isosurface crossing and that one fragment alpha-blends over the existing framebuffer (background/voxels/bonds), so a translucent region shows what was BEHIND the blob, never the blob's own far wall / interior / other agents inside it (the same single-surface Option-A rule as the spheres and voxels). True see-through would be front-to-back compositing through every crossing (or continuous absorption through the density field) — a real volume-rendering upgrade to META_FS, not a tweak. **Chromium gotcha (measured, load-bearing): `ctx.globalAlpha` is silently IGNORED on a `drawImage` routed through an SVG `url(#…)` filter** (a 0.5-alpha draw composites fully opaque; builtin `blur(0px)` and unfiltered draws honour it) — any per-draw alpha on a filtered blit MUST be baked into the filter chain instead. `ensureGooFilter`'s adopt path appends the fade stage to a pre-existing (HMR-injected) filter that lacks it.

### 3D viewport — feedback round 6 (inspect highlight not line, volumetric brush, ctrl-resize + ctrl-wheel, gizmo depth/letters, axes at origin)
- **Inspect cell HIGHLIGHT replaces the 2D connector line in 3D** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) + [gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): the dotted-yellow `InspectHoverLink` (whose `gridToScreen` projection is 2D-only and can't track a 3D voxel) is gated `!is3D` at BOTH render sites (the pinned-popover hover link + the sweep link). Instead, hovering an inspect popover highlights its cell IN the volume: a `useEffect` keyed on `hoveredInspectIdx` decodes the flat idx → `{layer,row,col}` into `inspectHighlight3dRef`; `draw()`'s 3D branch calls `r.setInspectCells(inspectHighlight3dRef.current)` every frame; the renderer draws them as **white** wireframe cubes via `renderHoverCells` — the same depth-test-OFF always-on-top path as the amber hover-footprint cursor, refactored into a shared `pushCellCube(out, c, col, h)` (inspect uses `h=0.6`, hover `0.56`). Hover-leave clears the ref → highlight vanishes. (NB for verification: `draw()` re-sets `setInspectCells(inspectHighlight3dRef.current)` every frame, so a manual `r.setInspectCells([...])` + `__sim3dRedraw()` is immediately overwritten — drive it through the popover's real `onMouseEnter`/`onMouseLeave`.) **2D inspector unchanged** (still draws the connector line + cell outline).
- **Volumetric brush — "Extrapolate plane (volumetric brush)" checkbox** (rendered ONLY when `is3D`, in the brush controls): `brush3dVolume` state + `brush3dVolumeRef` (synced via effect) + persisted in `genesisca_sim_settings` (object + dep array). OFF = the flat plane footprint (historical). ON = the brush shape becomes a 3D SOLID: `brushShapeOffsets3d(shape,…)` returns 3-tuples `[dr,dc,dl]` — circle→**sphere**, ring→**spherical shell**, rect→**box** (`bw col × bh row × bh layer`), line→a **ball** preview-dot. `currentStampOffsets3d()` (cached by a `v|shape|…` key) returns them in volume mode, else falls back to `currentStampOffsets()` (2-tuples). `mapStampToPlane` was widened to `(center, offsets: ReadonlyArray<readonly number[]>)` and now reads an optional 3rd `dl`, applying it to the plane's **FIXED** axis (z→layer, y→row, x→col), torus-wrapped — so `dl=0` (every 2D/flat call, where offsets are 2-tuples) is byte-identical to before. `paint3dRef`'s `stampAt` + the hover `footprintFor` both call `currentStampOffsets3d()`; `lineFootprint` extrudes the capsule along the fixed axis by `±⌊width/2⌋` in volume mode. Verified: a radius-2 circle paints an 81-cell sphere across 5 layers vs a 21-cell disc on 1 layer. (**Round 7** renames the checkbox to **"Volumetric Brush"** and adds a separate box **Depth** field — `boxDepth` param on `brushShapeOffsets3d`.)
- **Ctrl+LMB = resize, Shift+LMB = inspect, Ctrl+wheel ≠ zoom** (3D pointer effect — SUPERSEDES round 4's `(shift||ctrl||meta)`-inspect): `onDown` now routes `button 0 && (ctrl||meta)` → `active='resize'` (snapshots `resizeStart` = current w/h/radius/ringW/lineW) and `button 0 && shift` → `active='inspect'`. `onMove`'s new `resize` branch mirrors the 2D Ctrl-drag math (`±round(totDx/5)` primary size, `−round(totDy/5)` secondary, per active shape, clamped) → `setBrushRadius`/`setBrushRingWidth`/`setBrushLineWidth`/`setBrushW`+`setBrushH`, then `updateHover`+`draw`. `onWheel` early-returns on `ctrl||meta` (that combo cycles Input Mappings elsewhere) so it no longer ALSO zooms; plain wheel still zooms. Verified: Ctrl+drag grows the radius (2→14, no inspect popover); Ctrl+wheel leaves `cam.dist` unchanged (1.9→1.9), plain wheel changes it (1.9→2.09).
- **Gizmo gains DEPTH + axis LETTERS** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `renderGizmo`): the corner widget used to draw all 6 axis arms with no depth test, so a back arm (e.g. −X) could paint OVER a front one. Now it **scissor-clears only the gizmo viewport's depth** (`enable(SCISSOR_TEST)` + `scissor(…)` + `clear(DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT)` + `disable(SCISSOR_TEST)` + `enable(DEPTH_TEST)`) and draws the axes + endpoint points with **DEPTH TEST ON** (front occludes back), then draws the axis **letters** (C=col/+X, R=row/+Y, D=depth/+Z — `static GIZMO_LABELS` + stroke `static GLYPHS`) with depth OFF, an identity MVP, projecting each positive tip to NDC (×1.22 outward) and **skipping back-facing letters** (`facing < −0.15`). GL state (scissor/depth/viewport) is restored so subsequent passes are unaffected. (**Round 7** reworks the letters — drops the back-face cull, uses a constant radial offset + uniform size — and updates the label dirs to **C→+X, R→-Y, D→-Z**; the depth-correct axis stub drawing here is unchanged.)
- **Main Axes drawn from the ORIGIN, not the volume centre** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `renderOverlays`, the `if (this.viz.axes)` block): the toggleable RGB axes now start at cell `(0,0,0)`'s world centre (`-hx, -hy, +hz` in the Z-up remap, since layer 0 sits at the top) and grow toward **+col** (red, `+x`), **+row** (green, `+y`), **+depth** (blue, downward `−z`, because layer increases downward), each capped with a 2-pronged arrowhead — so they mark WHERE the origin is and WHICH WAY row/col/depth increase, instead of crossing through the middle of the volume. (**Round 7** flips **+row → -Y** so the top view matches the 2D CA — the origin is now at `(-hx, +hy, +hz)` and +row grows toward -Y/screen-down.)
- **Verified** (runtime via `window.__sim3d*` hooks + readPixels + screenshot): inspect popover opens in 3D and hovering it highlights the cell (2157-byte frame diff) / leaving clears it (0-byte); volumetric sphere 81 cells across 5 layers vs flat disc 21/1; Ctrl-drag radius 2→14 with 0 inspect popovers; Ctrl+wheel dist unchanged, plain wheel zooms; Axes + Gizmo toggles render with no GL/console errors; the origin axes (red/green/blue from the corner) + Z-up gizmo render as intended in the screenshot. tsc + build clean. 2D paint/inspect paths byte-identical (dl defaults 0; the new effect + checkbox are 3D-gated).

### 3D viewport — feedback round 7 (top view = 2D orientation, depth-POV roll, gizmo letters, sweep inspect, "Volumetric Brush" + box depth)
- **Top-down view now matches the 2D CA — row axis FLIPPED to world -Y** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): the world remap is now **col→+X (right), row→-Y (DOWN the screen), depth/layer→-Z (into the screen, layer 0 on top)**. A camera can't mirror, so getting col-right AND row-down in the top view required flipping the ROW axis from +Y to -Y (`world_y = halfY - row`). EVERY world-remap site flips row consistently: the `VS` + `PICK_VS` shader centres (`uHalf.y - row`), `pushCellCube` / `sortBackToFront` (`cy = hy - row`), `pickOnPlane` (the `'y'` plane = `hy - pos`, inverse `row = round(hy - wy)`), `renderOverlays` axes (origin `oy = +hy`; +row → -Y), `renderBrushPlane` (`'y'` plane `y = hy - pos`). **SUPERSEDES round 6's "+row → +Y" axes line above.** Renderer-only → **ZERO 2D impact** (2D uses the Canvas2D path). Verified: top-view `pickOnPlane` at screen-centre / +80px-right / +80px-down → col increases right, row increases down, all on layer 0.
- **Camera roll at the ±Depth POV** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `setCamera` + [SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) `setPov`): looking down/up the Z (depth) axis makes WORLD_UP (+Z) parallel to the view → `lookAt` degenerate. `setCamera` swaps in **up = `[0, fwd.z>0 ? -1 : 1, 0]`** when `|camForward.z| > 0.999` (top → up +Y → col-right/row-down; bottom → up -Y), else WORLD_UP. `setPov` sets the depth POV pitch to **±π/2 EXACTLY** (was ±1.49) so the snap reaches the pole-up branch; the general orbit clamps `|pitch| ≤ 1.5` (`|fwd.z| ≤ 0.997 < 0.999`) so orbiting KEEPS WORLD_UP — the Y-up only kicks in at the exact snap (a deliberate roll "pop" between the snapped top and the first orbit frame, accepted). `setPov` also changed the x/y POVs to **look ALONG the clicked axis tip** (`dir = -tipV`), so clicking the **D** gizmo tip (at -Z = the +depth direction) gives the **top / 2D-matching** view.
- **Gizmo letters reworked** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `renderGizmo` + `GIZMO_LABELS`): label directions follow the new orientation (**C→+X, R→-Y, D→-Z**). The letter rendering **dropped the back-face cull** (that was the "letters disappear at some angles" bug — all 3 now always draw), nudges each glyph a **constant radial offset** beyond its tip (fixes off-centre), and uses a **uniform NDC size** (`sz 0.26`, `pushOut 0.24`). Verified: label-tint pixels present in the gizmo region at multiple camera angles (no disappearing).
- **3D sweep inspect — Shift+LMB DRAG** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) `sweepPick3d` + the 3D pointer effect): holding Shift+LMB and dragging shows a **single transient popover** (reuses the 2D `sweepInspector` state) that tracks the **front voxel** under the cursor (colour-id `pick()`), refreshes its data live, and **highlights** the cell (`inspectHighlight3dRef`) — quick region investigation without pinning a popover per cell. Release **without** moving PINS (the round-6 `openInspect3dRef` path); release **after** a drag DISCARDS (mirrors the 2D sweep inspector's `!moved` rule). The popover anchors at the press point (no 2D connector line in 3D). **Inspect mode + AGENT sweep (2026-07):** the toolbar ⓘ Inspect toggle makes plain LMB inspect (2D + 3D — the pointer handlers check `e.shiftKey || inspectModeRef.current`; the 2D cell-sweep's release-Shift cancel is waived while inspect mode holds the gesture — previously the first drag move cancelled the sweep under the button). AGENTS sweep too, on BOTH gestures + both dimensions: pressing an agent opens the inspector immediately (the popover doubles as the drag's transient, anchored at the press point) and dragging re-targets it to whichever agent is under the cursor (2D `agentSweep*Ref`s; 3D `active='inspectAgent'` + effect-local `agentSweepStartId`/`agentSweepMoved`); the discard rule is AGENT-CHANGE-based (not pixel-based): release without ever re-targeting a different agent keeps it pinned, a sweep across other agents discards. NB verifying with synthetic PointerEvents: `set/releasePointerCapture` THROW on a fake pointerId and abort the handler mid-way — neutralize them (`glc.setPointerCapture = () => {}`) before driving the 3D canvas. The round-6 hover-highlight effect (keyed on `hoveredInspectIdx`) doesn't run during a sweep (that idx stays null), so the two don't fight; the sweep clears the highlight on release. 2D + 3D are mutually exclusive (the 3D pointer effect early-returns `if (!is3D)`; 2D handlers live on the `display:none` 2D canvas), so sharing the sweep refs is safe. Verified: down → 1 popover, drag → follows (cell updates, stays 1), release-after-drag → 0.
- **"Volumetric Brush" (renamed) + box DEPTH field** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)): round 6's "Extrapolate plane (volumetric brush)" checkbox is renamed **"Volumetric Brush"**. The volumetric **box** (rect shape) gained its own **Depth** field (`brushBoxDepth` state/ref/sync-effect/persist; shown when `is3D && brush3dVolume && rect`, max `gridDepth.current||simDepth`) so the box's LAYER extent is independent of the row size (H): `brushShapeOffsets3d` takes a `boxDepth` param (`halfD = ⌊(boxDepth-1)/2⌋`, same off-centre bias as W/H), threaded through `currentStampOffsets3d` (+ the stamp cache key `v|shape|bw|bh|radius|ringW|boxD`). Sphere/shell/tube unchanged. The box-depth rides the SAME volumetric paint pipeline already runtime-verified for the sphere.
- **Verified**: `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; top-view orientation, gizmo letters, and sweep inspect runtime-confirmed; the Depth field appears for rect+Volumetric; 2D paths untouched (all new behaviour is 3D-gated / renderer-only).

### 3D viewport — Lighting controls + Cell gaps toggle (branch `sim_agent_fixes`)
Plan + mockup: [docs/PLAN_LIBRARY_3D_VIEW_UX.md](docs/PLAN_LIBRARY_3D_VIEW_UX.md) / `.html` (also covers the Models Library browse tools). Render-layer only — zero compiler/worker/schema impact; 2D untouched.
- **Lighting** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): the voxel FS + agent-sphere FS no longer hardcode `L=normalize(0.4,0.8,0.6)` / `0.45+0.55·n·L` — they take uniforms `uLightDir`/`uAmbient`/`uDiffuse`/`uSpecular`(+`uViewDir` on the voxel FS), shade = `ambient + diffuse·max(0,n·L)` + an optional white Blinn-Phong `specular·max(0,n·H)³²`. **`Light3D`** (exported, + `DEFAULT_LIGHT3D` which reproduces the historical shade EXACTLY — world mode, the same normalized vector, .45/.55/0): `{ mode:'camera'|'world', bx, by, wx, wy, wz, ambient, diffuse, specular }`. `setLight()` + the per-frame `lightWorldDir()`: **camera** mode combines the ball position (bx,by, implied bz=√(1−bx²−by²) toward the viewer) with the CURRENT camera basis each frame (light rides the view — headlight/matcap); **world** mode returns the stored unit vector (scene-fixed — sun). `setLightUniforms` runs inside `setCommonUniforms` + `setSphereUniforms` (null locations on the pick programs no-op per GL spec). `cameraBasis` is now EXPORTED — SimulatorView's `applyLightBall`/`setLightMode` convert a world-mode drag (and the camera→world switch, which freezes the current view-relative dir so switching NEVER jumps the shading) through it. UI ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) 3D View panel): a **Lighting** block — [LightBallWidget.tsx](src/simulator/LightBallWidget.tsx) (an SVG "sun position" ball: radial-gradient highlight + dot track the light; window-level pointer listeners like ClipIntervalSlider) + Ambient/Light/Shine range sliders + View/World mode buttons + Reset. State `light3d` (sanitized on load via `sanitizeLight3d`) persists in `genesisca_sim_settings.light3d`. **NB the state is declared with the other persisted view options ABOVE the settings-persist effect** (declaring it in the 3D-controls block below TDZ-crashes the persist dep array); the ball/mode HELPERS stay in the 3D block (they close over `cam3dRef`). Verified: ambient slider brightens; Reset restores the baseline sum bit-exactly; ball drag changes shading with identical lit-pixel count; View→World switch = 0 pixel change; orbit-away-and-back in World mode ≠ the original (light stayed put) — the mode semantics proof.
- **Cell gaps** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `setCellGaps(on)`): the voxel `uCubeScale` is no longer the hardcoded 0.92 — ON (the historical look) = 0.92; OFF (**default since the occlusion round** — flush cubes = the seamless-solid look AND the mode where buried-cell culling engages; the state default is `saved.cellGaps3d === true` so an explicitly saved ON is respected) = **1.001**, deliberately NOT 1.0 (exactly-coplanar shared faces of adjacent cubes z-fight along seam edges; the 0.001 overlap keeps every interior face strictly behind a neighbour's outer face → seamless solid volume). The pick pass shares the uniform via `setCommonUniforms`, so clicking "between" cells matches what's drawn. UI: a "Cell gaps" checkbox next to Alpha blend (the 3D analogue of the 2D gridlines toggle), persisted as `genesisca_sim_settings.cellGaps3d`, **gated on `gridCellsOn`** — it only affects the CA-grid voxels, so it's hidden for an **agents-only** model (no grid to render); Alpha blend by contrast stays (it also drives the agent-sphere blend via `setAgentAlphaBlend`). Verified: gaps OFF fills more pixels (156716→160497 lit on Life3D), back ON restores exactly; hidden on Morphogenesis — 3D Tissue (agents-only), shown on Life3D.

#### Global lighting — cast shadows + occlusion AO (opt-in; branch `sim_agent_fixes`)
Two opt-in "global lighting" toggles so cells/agents shade EACH OTHER instead of each surface being lit only by its own normal (the reported "each cell/agent is independently lit" flatness). Both DEFAULT OFF → **byte-identical to the historical shade** (verified: off = exact baseline luminance; strength 0 == off). Render-layer only ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) + the [SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) Lighting panel) — zero compiler/worker/schema impact; 2D untouched. `Light3D` gained `{ shadows, shadowStrength, ao, aoStrength }` (defaults false/0.6/false/0.7; `sanitizeLight3d` clamps them; persisted in `genesisca_sim_settings.light3d`).
- **Cast shadows (shadow map)** — voxels + agents both CAST and RECEIVE. A depth-only pass renders the cubes + agent sphere impostors from the light's ORTHO POV into a `DEPTH_COMPONENT24` `sampler2DShadow` texture (`renderShadowMap`, first in `render()`, no-op when off); the voxel + sphere FS PCF-sample it (shared `SHADOW_GLSL` `shadowFactor(fragWorld, ndl)` — 3×3 taps over hardware-2×2 compare) to attenuate the diffuse+specular. **The light matrix** (`computeLightMVP`) fits the volume's bounding SPHERE (radius R from the origin-centred world), eye at `2R·L`, ortho `±R`, near/far `0.5R/3.5R` — stable at ANY light angle; up-vector degeneracy guarded (`|L.z|>0.99 → [0,1,0]`). **Two dedicated depth programs**: `CUBE_SHADOW_VS/FS` (decodes the cell centre EXACTLY like the main VS — col→+X/row→-Y/layer→-Z) and `SPHERE_SHADOW_VS/FS` (a LIGHT-facing billboard — axes ⟂ L via cross products — raycast to the light-facing sphere hemisphere + `gl_FragDepth`). Both clip-aware (a clipped-open view's interior is lit as if the removed shell weren't casting). **Scale-relative bias** `uShadowBias = 0.9/(3R)` (a ≈1-cell world bias mapped into the light's [0,1] depth range) + slope term — avoids acne on small grids AND peter-panning on huge ones. `SHADOW_SIZE=2048`, on `SHADOW_TEX_UNIT=1`; a 1×1 dummy depth-compare texture (`ensureDummyShadowTex`) is ALWAYS bound to unit 1 so the `sampler2DShadow` stays valid when shadows are off (the FS branches out on `uShadowEnabled=0`). Depth-only FBO completeness is checked → `shadowUnsupported` quietly disables shadows on a driver that can't do it. `sampler2DShadow` needs an explicit `precision highp sampler2DShadow;` in GLSL ES 3.00 (the compile error that first surfaced).
- **Ambient occlusion (voxel occupancy)** — each cell is darkened by how many of its 6 face-neighbours are filled (crevices/interiors → darker), computed in `uploadColors` INSIDE the existing per-step cell scan (an incremental `(col,row,layer)` walk + 6 bounded neighbour alpha reads, ONLY when AO is on — the off path stays as cheap as before). Stored in a PARALLEL `aoBuf` (attrib 4 on the cube VAO; the pick/shadow cube programs don't declare it → ignored) and applied to the AMBIENT term (`uAmbient*(1 - aoStrength*vAO)`), so shadows own the diffuse and AO owns the ambient. `sortBackToFront` reorders `aoData` in step with the alpha-blend instance reorder. `aoBuf` is kept finite across every off→on→off + capacity-grow path (grow allocates zeros; on = refresh; off+no-grow = skip, ×0 in the FS). Because AO is BAKED at upload, toggling it forces a re-upload (`prevAoRef` resets `lastUploadedColors3dRef` in the light3d effect — colours are unchanged so `draw()` would otherwise skip `uploadColors`). Agents get their "no longer independently lit" from the cast shadows, not occupancy AO (spheres already read as 3D; a screen-space / agent-cluster AO is a possible follow-up).
- **UI**: two rows (Shadows / Occlusion) in the 3D-View Lighting panel — a checkbox + a strength slider each (dimmed when off), below the View/World mode buttons. **The Occlusion row is gated on `gridCellsOn`** (hidden for an agents-only model, like Cell gaps): occupancy AO is computed from CELL face-neighbours in `uploadColors`, so it does nothing for agents — Shadows stays (agents cast + receive).
- **Verified on the GPU (readPixels luminance; screenshots time out on this heavy page)**: all shaders compile + the renderer inits; AO on darkens Life3D meanLum 97.82→85.57 (same pixel count) and off restores EXACTLY 97.82; shadows scale with strength (97.82 → 95.09 @0.6 → 93.32 @1.0) and off restores 97.82; a 200-agent dense cluster receives shadows (scales 0→1); zero console errors throughout. tsc + `npm run build` clean. (The planned adversarial-review workflow's agents hit the session limit — replaced by a manual adversarial self-review across the same dimensions; no defects.)

#### Agent Metaballs — implicit-surface agent rendering, 2D + 3D (opt-in; branch `sim_agent_fixes`)
Render the agent population as a **fused implicit surface** (Blender-metaball semantics) instead of discrete circles / sphere impostors — each agent contributes a Wyvill falloff `w = (1 − (d/R)²)³` over `R = influence · radius`, the fields SUM, and the surface is the isosurface at `threshold`, so overlapping agents bulge toward each other and MERGE (the natural look for tissues/morphogenesis). **Purely a RENDER mode** — picking/brushing/inspect (the sphere pick FBO), bonds, physics, engine, compilers, schema all untouched; sprite-agents are EXCLUDED from the field and keep drawing as billboards on top. Plan + deviations: [docs/PLAN_AGENT_METABALLS.md](docs/PLAN_AGENT_METABALLS.md) (+ `.html` mockup). DEFAULT OFF → byte-identical baseline (verified: off = exact sphere lit-pixel counts).
- **Config**: `Metaballs3D { enabled, influence 1–3, threshold 0.02–0.9, resolution 1|2|4 }` + `DEFAULT_METABALLS3D` + **`metaballAutoThreshold(influence) = (1 − 1/influence²)³`** — the threshold at which a LONE agent renders at EXACTLY its own sphere radius (verified pixel-exact: 585 = 585 px) — all exported from [gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts). ONE shared, persisted user setting (`genesisca_sim_settings.agentMetaballs`, state `agentMetaballs` + `sanitizeAgentMetaballs` in [SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx)) drives BOTH the 3D and 2D views. UI: a **Metaballs** block in the 3D View panel (checkbox + Influence/Threshold sliders + a ⟲ auto-threshold button + a 1×/2×/4× Detail control, agent models only) and a **Metaballs** block in the 2D agent common-controls ("Fuse agents into blobs" + the two sliders).
- **3D = bake a density field into a 3D texture, then raymarch it** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts) `bakeMetaballField`/`renderMetaballs` + `META_VS`/`META_FS`): the CPU splat reads the **packed `agentInstData`** (already alive-compacted, colours 0..1, and sprite-agents flagged by a NEGATIVE radius — NB the SNAPSHOT's radius is always positive, so a snapshot-based bake would misclassify sprites) into an RGBA8 `TEXTURE_3D` over the agents' influence bbox (A = Σw/`META_F_MAX`(2.0), RGB = density-weighted colour → colours blend smoothly across fused tissue); the fullscreen-quad FS reconstructs the world ray from `inv(MVP)`, slab-tests the field box, marches (≤512 steps, half-voxel step) to the first threshold crossing, bisects ×5, shades with the SAME lighting model incl. `shadowFactor()` (the blob RECEIVES cast shadows; the sphere casters approximate the blob's own shadow), and writes `gl_FragDepth` with the SPHERE_FS formula so it depth-interleaves with voxels/bonds/sprites. Clip interval reads as density-0 → the blob is cut OPEN (matching the voxel cut). **The bake is LAZY on a `metaDirty` flag** (set by `setMetaballs` param changes / `uploadAgents` / `clearAgents`) — camera-only frames re-render without re-baking (measured 0.3 ms vs 9–37 ms bakes at 1500 agents); NO SimulatorView snapshot invalidation needed. **Field cap** ~2M voxels (`META_MAX_VOXELS`, ≈128³) with a CONTINUOUS per-axis effective resolution; **torus worlds bake the FULL world with modulo-wrapped splats** (verified: a seam-hugging agent's blob renders on both sides). **The mirroring gotcha**: `uFieldA`/`uFieldB` are the SIGNED world coords of the field's cell-min/cell-max corners — NOT a numeric min/max (the Z-up remap negates row/layer; per-axis `(p−A)/(B−A)` with a negative span is exactly what keeps the texture un-mirrored, and the slab test is order-robust). Texture unit **2** (0 = sprite atlas, 1 = shadow map), bound AFTER `setLightUniforms` (which resets the active unit to 0).
- **2D = an approximate "gooey" filter** ([SimulatorView.tsx](src/simulator/SimulatorView.tsx) `drawAgentsOverlay` + `ensureGooFilter`): non-sprite agent discs draw into an offscreen scratch (`gooScratchRef`, drawn-to only — never `getImageData`'d), then ONE blit through `ctx.filter = 'url(#genesisca-agent-goo)'` — an injected SVG filter of feGaussianBlur (σ = `(influence−1)·avgRadPx·0.85`) + a steep feColorMatrix alpha threshold. **The threshold is anchored to the blurred-disc PEAK alpha** (`1 − exp(−r²/2σ²)`) — without this, lone agents VANISH at high influence because blur flattens their peak below a fixed threshold (found in verification); with it, fusion is monotonic in influence (verified: 5 discs → 1 blob) and the full threshold range stays usable. Sprite-agents draw normally on top (a second `'sprites'` stamp pass); falls back to plain circles when `ctx.filter` is unsupported. `ensureGooFilter` ADOPTS an existing `#genesisca-agent-goo` element by id before creating one — a re-evaluated module (Vite HMR) must not append a duplicate id, since `url(#…)` resolves the FIRST match and the duplicate's params would never reach the canvas (a real debugging trap hit during verification).
- **Verification recipe** (screenshots time out on the 3D page — use `readPixels` via `window.__sim3dRenderer()` + direct `r.setMetaballs(...); r.render()` calls, and count connected components of the lit mask WITH OVERLAYS OFF — the bounds box/gizmo lines otherwise contaminate the component count): off = exact baseline; influence sweep monotonic; threshold monotonic; comps drop to 1 on fusion; pick id identical on/off.

### Post-PR8 — camera/viewport overhaul + interaction-plane brush (feedback round 1)
- **Z-up Blender camera** ([gl3d.ts](src/simulator/render/gl3d.ts)): the world remap is now **Z-up** — XY is the horizon (the 2D-CA plane), `z = uHalf.z - layer` (layer increases DOWNWARD), so a top-down view shows the grid like a 2D CA. `Camera3D` = `{ yaw, pitch, dist, target:[x,y,z] }` (target replaces panX/panY). `cameraBasis()` derives forward/right/up; **`panCamera(cam, dx, dy, scale)`** (exported) pans the target along the camera's right/up axes (screen-space pan, tracks the viewpoint). Input scheme (SimulatorView pointer effect): **MMB-drag OR Alt+LMB-drag = orbit**, **Shift+(MMB/Alt+LMB) = pan**, **wheel = zoom**, **plain LMB = brush** (interaction plane), **Ctrl/Cmd+LMB click = inspect**. (LMB is the brush, so orbit is on MMB/Alt+LMB, Blender-style. **Round 3 adds RMB-drag = pan.**)
- **Scene overlays + gizmo** (`Viz3D = {axes, grid, bounds, gizmo}`): a 2nd unlit line program draws toggleable RGB **axes**, an XY floor **grid** (stepped if >100 cells/axis), and the volume **bounds** box; a corner **gizmo** viewport shows the axes rotating with the camera (orthographic, `camDir`). `setViz()` per frame from `viz3dRef`. (**Round 3 makes the gizmo clickable** — 6 ± endpoints snap to the main POVs — see "feedback round 3".)
- **Clip plane gained a `'camera'` axis** — discards fragments past a plane perpendicular to the view direction (`uClipForward` = camForward); peels toward the viewer. X/Y/Z unchanged.
- **Interaction-plane brush** (the 3D brush): `pickOnPlane(cssX, cssY, cssW, cssH, axis, pos)` unprojects the cursor through `inv(MVP)` to a world ray and intersects the axis-aligned slice plane at grid index `pos`, returning the nearest in-bounds `{layer,row,col}` (or null). LMB-drag ray-traces onto the plane → `paint3dRef`, which **stamps the full brush shape on the plane + interpolates the drag** (see PR8 "Brush shape on the plane"). Controlled by the **Brush plane** toggle (axis + position slider). `mat4Invert`/`unproject`/`mat4Ortho` added.
- **Auto-orbit** (`orbit3d {on, speed}`): a rAF loop spins `cam.yaw` while enabled + 3D + visible. **Collapsible 3D View panel** (`controls3dOpen`) — Reset view, Axes/Grid/Bounds/Gizmo toggles, Auto-orbit+speed, Clip plane (X/Y/Z/View + slider), Brush plane (axis + slider), Alpha blend.
- **Verified** (readPixels): orbit/pan change the image; axes/grid/bounds/gizmo add pixels; camera-axis clip reduces pixels; `pickOnPlane` resolves the correct cell for top-down + 3/4 views on X/Y/Z planes (passing the drawing-buffer dims — the GL canvas's CSS layout size can read 0 in a headless eval, but the real pointer handler uses `getBoundingClientRect`).
- **Live 3D neighbourhood preview** ([NeighborhoodPreview3D.tsx](src/modeler/panels/NeighborhoodPreview3D.tsx)): a small orbiting canvas in the 3D neighbourhood editor that reuses `Gl3DRenderer` to show the centre cell (amber) + neighbour offsets (green) inside a bounds box (auto-orbit + drag + wheel). Rebuilds a synthetic `(2·maxAbs+1)³` colours buffer on `coords3d` change. Verified: the preview canvas lays out + renders (295×170, GL context constructs clean), Generate (Moore r1) → 26 cells reflected.

### PR9 — save/load + indicators + inspector 3D consumer-scan (done)
- **Save/load** ([fileOperations.ts](src/model/fileOperations.ts)): the worker getState payload echoes `depth` ([sim.worker.ts](src/simulator/engine/sim.worker.ts)); `serializeSimState`/`serializePreset` write `gridDepth` (always) + `depth` (inside the grid block); `readStateFile` normalizes `depth`/`gridDepth` → 1 for legacy files. `applySimulationState` deserializes at `total = W*H*depth` (a 3D state would otherwise truncate to its first layer), threads `depth` through `dimsFromState`/`dimsChanged` (adapting the model + flipping it to `dimension:'3d'` when `depth>1`), and the hard dim-validation rejects a depth mismatch with a `W×H×D` message (3rd dim shown only when either side is a volume). The preset-load dim check mirrors it. **`gridDepth.current`** is set beside `gridWidth/Height.current` (init + applySimulationState).
- **Spatial `'layers'` axis**: `computeSpatialIndicators` ([sim.worker.ts](src/simulator/engine/sim.worker.ts)) accepts `xAxis==='layers'` (axisLen = depth; the posBin decode is now 3D-correct — layer=⌊i/WH⌋, row/col within the layer — behaviour-identical in 2D); the `hasSpatialIndicators` gate + the post-loop linked-skip + the WebGPU-reduction-skip all include `'layers'`. `IndicatorSpatialChart` widens `axis` to `'layers'` (axisName 'layer'); `IndicatorDisplay` gained a `gridDepth` prop (axisLength = depth for layers) + its three `isSpatial` checks include `'layers'`; the IndicatorsPanelSection X-axis dropdown offers **Layers (spatial, 3D)** only when `dimension==='3d'`. **Verified**: a per-layer frequency indicator's `true` series matches an independent re-bin by ⌊i/WH⌋ with 0 mismatches.
- **Inspector** ([InspectCellPopover.tsx](src/simulator/InspectCellPopover.tsx)): the `neighborIndex` value decode was 2-axis `(dr, dc)` in PR9; **PR10 made it dimension-aware** — 3D models decode `(dr, dc, dl)` via `unpackNI3` (an `is3d` prop threaded from SimulatorView).
- **Verified**: 3D getState `depth=24`; serialized `gridDepth/depth=24` + the `alive` buffer base64-decodes to the full 13824 bytes (not truncated to W*H); spatial-layers 0 mismatches. `ATTR_TYPE_MAP` needs no change (a longer buffer base64's automatically).

### PR10 — 3-axis NeighborIndex offsets + un-gate the NI family in 3D (done; lockstep)
- **Why:** PR4 gated the whole `neighborIndex` family off in 3D because the packed codec carried only two 16-bit `(dr, dc)` halves. PR10 adds a 3-axis codec and un-gates the family, so offset-based neighbour access (the imperative `getAllNeighborIndexes → filter/pick/forEach → get/setNeighborAttributeByIndex` style) works on the 3D lattice. User decision: "enable 3D offsets, defer the rename" (the `neighborIndex` type id + node labels are unchanged; only 3D support added).
- **Codec** ([niCodec.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/niCodec.ts)): 3D packs three sign-extended **10-bit** fields (`dr<<20 | dc<<10 | dl`, ±511/axis); 2D keeps the verbatim 16-bit codec → **2D output byte-identical**. `INVALID_NI = 0x80000000` stays valid in BOTH (bit 31 free in 3D's bits 0-29; consumers compare the bit pattern before decoding). New: `packNI3`/`unpackNI3`; the emit helpers `niDrExpr`/`niDcExpr`(+`is3d`) / `niDlExpr` / `niPackExpr`(+`is3d`,`dlExpr`) / `niCellExprStmts`(+`is3d` → a 3D branch using `_layer`/`D`/`WH`). `is3dModelLike(model)` — a lightweight 3D check for node files (avoids importing `is3dModel` from compile.ts → circular).
- **Per-model codec selection (single source of truth per target):** JS = `CompileContext.is3d` (= `is3dModel(model)`), threaded into every NI node's `compile(...ctx)`. WASM = `ctx.layerLocalIdx >= 0`. WebGPU = `ctx.layout.gridDepth > 1`. All three resolve the cell via the layer (`(nl*H+nr)*W+nc`, torus wrap or constant→`total` sentinel).
- **JS** ([compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/compile.ts)): pre-pass `packCoord(nbr, slot)` packs from `coords3d`+`packNI3` in 3D (for getAllNeighborIndexes / getNeighborIndexesByTags / neighborIndexFromTag); `niCellExprStmts` callers (get/getNeighborsAttr/filter/set/markCellUpdated/moveSelfToNeighbor) pass `ctx?.is3d`; `neighborIndexFromOffset` / `breakDownNeighborIndex` / `flipNeighborIndex` use the dim-aware helpers.
- **WASM** ([wasm/compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/wasm/compile.ts)): `pushNiCellIdx` 3D branch; `neighborIndexFromOffset`/`breakDown`/`flip` + `getAllNeighborIndexes` emit the 10-bit codec; the tag pre-pass now packs `_resolvedTagIndexes`/`_resolvedPacked` 3D-aware (this also **fixed a latent bug**: the WASM-only path emitted bare *slot indices* instead of packed NIs — masked whenever the JS pre-pass resolved the config first).
- **WebGPU** ([webgpu/encoder.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/webgpu/encoder.ts) + [compile.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/webgpu/compile.ts)): `nbrCellIdxFromNi` 3D form in the encoder; same three node emitters + `getAllNeighborIndexes` 3D. (Tag NIs read the JS/WASM-resolved packed config, as before.)
- **Un-gate**: removed `requirements.lattice2d` from all 13 NI nodes; the flag is kept as generic 2D-only infra (no current users — comment updated in types.ts). `neighborIndexFromOffset` + `breakDownNeighborIndex` gain a `dl` port, hidden in 2D via `hiddenPorts(_config, model) => is3dModelLike(model) ? [] : ['dl']`. `breakDownNeighborIndex`'s `dl` resolves via the multi-output `_v<id>_dl` convention (already in MULTI_OUTPUT_TYPES); `flipNeighborIndex` preserves dl (the flips are XY-plane). The `neighborIndex` value-type in Switch/Compare needs no change — it compares the packed bit pattern (dimension-agnostic) and now has real sources in 3D.
- **UI**: `NeighborIndexValuePicker` + `NeighborIndexDefaultEditor` ([NeighborIndexDefaultEditor.tsx](src/modeler/panels/NeighborIndexDefaultEditor.tsx)) take an `is3d` prop → pack/decode 3 axes + a `dl` (layer) stepper, grid hint-membership per layer (`coords3d`), 10-bit clamp (±511). Threaded from AttributesPanelContent (default/boundary/undefined editors), ManualBrushPanel, the simulator model-attr panel, and the cell inspector (`unpackNI3`) — all keyed on `dimension==='3d' && (gridDepth??1)>1`.
- **Verified**: tsc + full build clean; cross-target compile parity via the harness (all three emit the 10-bit codec; 2D byte-identical); JS direct-runtime 0 mismatches on a layer-up shift; and the **real worker on all three targets** — a "copy the cell one layer below" rule (`neighborIndexFromOffset(0,0,-1) → getNeighborAttributeByIndex`) rose a seeded ring exactly one layer per step on WASM (L0→L3), JS (L0→L5), and WebGPU (L0→L4), confirming cross-target 3D-NI runtime parity. (This was driven by a throwaway `Shift3D` demo model + a `gen-shift3d.mjs` generator — both **removed** afterward as too specific to ship in the Models Library; the NI-3D code is regression-covered by the dev harness, which needs no shipped model.)
