## Agent Capability Profiles (branch `absorb_old_automatosgt`)

Decompose the monolithic agent engine into **opt-in capability modules** so a model declares only the capabilities it needs and its EDITOR SURFACE (palette nodes, Behaviour-Step ports, agent Edit-panel rows, inspector fields) shows only its paradigm's machinery — a social-graph or particle author never sees morphogenesis nodes. Universality by **composition**, not by piling every feature onto one fixed agent. Spec/audit trail: [docs/HANDOFF_AGENT_CAPABILITY_PROFILES.md](docs/HANDOFF_AGENT_CAPABILITY_PROFILES.md) (authoritative) + [PLAN](docs/PLAN_AGENT_CAPABILITY_PROFILES.md) + [ANALYSIS](docs/ANALYSIS_AUTOMATOSGT_AGENTS.md). **STEP 0 + STEP 1 shipped (the entire clarity win, editor-surface); STEP 3 (Bonds SoA gate) + the physics-capability ENGINE wiring (Collision / Bonds=Physics / Growth now gate real engine behaviour — the honest-controls pass) shipped on top, all byte-identical for existing files; the remaining SoA memory-compute gating (STEP 4/6) + net-new STEP 5 nodes are the secondary win.**

### The honest core (always allocated) + the gate-able capabilities
- **Core (never SoA-gated before the deferred STEP 6):** the slot **id** (= the array index) + `alive` + `colors` + position (`x,y[,z]` + the `Next` double-buffer) + velocity (`vx,vy[,vz]`) + force (`forceX,forceY[,forceZ]`) + `radius` + the always-on i32 reductions `lineage`/`epoch`/`bondCount` + the user's agent attributes. **Motion & Body are always-allocated in v1** (there is no `Static` integrator path yet) — their toggle is a palette/port gate only; SoA-gating them is the deferred XL STEP 6.
- **Capabilities (gate-able on top of core):** Motion (static/velocity/force), Body, Collision (off/**soft**=springy repulsion force / **positional**=rigid no-overlap constraint — two GENUINELY different models), Bonds (off/data/physics), Auto-bond, Growth, Division, Lifespan, Population (birth/death), Sensing, Orientation, Field coupling, Appearance. **The PHYSICS capabilities (Collision / Bonds=Physics / Growth) now drive the ENGINE, not just the editor surface** — see "Physics-capability engine wiring" + "Positional (hard) collision" below.

### The profile is the single source of truth ([src/model/agentCapabilities.ts](src/model/agentCapabilities.ts))
- **Schema** ([types.ts](src/model/types.ts)): `AgentCapabilities` + `MotionMode`/`CollisionMode`/`BondsMode`; `CenterBasedConfig.agentCapabilities?` (additive — absent ⇒ inferred).
- **7 presets** (`AGENT_PRESETS`): Particle · Boids · Vivarium · Morphogenesis · Social Graph · CA-on-Agents. `Full ≡ Morphogenesis` (deep-equal — a STEP 1 gate). `matchAgentPreset` flips to `'custom'` on any edit that stops matching.
- **Mode-aware dependency closure** (`computeCapabilityClosure`): auto-enables hard deps (Collision=soft ⇒ Body + Motion=Force; Bonds=physics ⇒ Motion=Force; Growth/Division ⇒ Body; Auto-bond ⇒ Bonds=physics; Population.Birth ⇒ Motion). `applyCapabilityEdit` ALSO cascades REMOVALS (Body off ⇒ drop Growth/Division/Collision; Motion below Force ⇒ demote physics bonds / soft collision).
- **Node→capability requirement table** (`AGENT_NODE_REQUIREMENT`, `nodeSatisfiesCapabilities`): the ONE source consumed by BOTH the palette gate AND the migration widening. A node not listed is core/universal (Behaviour Step, Get Self Position, Get/Set Agent Attribute-by-id, the array-tier plumbing) — always available when Agents is on. Only nodes that should VANISH when their capability is off are listed (Divide Agent→division, Apply Force→motion=force, Get Radius→body, Form Bond/For Each Bond→bonds, Secrete To Field→fieldCoupling, …).
- **Usage-widened migration** (`inferAgentProfile` / `migrateAgentCapabilities`, wired into LOAD_MODEL + `migrateForHarness`): **minimal base + engine-physics signal (`usesBondingPhysics` ⇒ collision/body/force, `autoBond` ⇒ physics bonds) + node-usage widen + Body-for-any-moving-model, then closure.** **DELIBERATE DEVIATION from the handoff's config-base-Full** — this is provably safe (the widen uses the SAME requirement table as the gate, so it can NEVER hide a node the graph uses) AND honest/tight (a maxBonds>0 model that never touches bonds no longer shows the whole morphogenesis palette). Verified: shipped Boids infers `motion/body/sensing` only; Tissue infers full physics+division. A fresh Agents-topology enable seeds the friendly `defaultAgentCapabilities()` = Boids.
- **Footprint estimate** (`estimateAgentFootprint`): the per-agent byte readout enumerating the SAME field groups STEP 3's profile-driven `computeAgentMemoryLayout` will gate (v1 is a display-only what-if — the engine still allocates the full struct).

### The three enforcement gates (all read the resolved profile)
1. **Palette** ([nodeValidation.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/nodeValidation.ts) `isNodeAvailable`): hides a capability-off node from palette / quick-add / connection-drop (within the existing `bondGraph`+Agents-graph branch).
2. **Badge** (`detectCapabilityRequirements` → CaNode amber `!`): INFORMATIONAL in v1 (the compiler still emits unconditionally, so a placed violator keeps working; the badge guides the user). Covers macro internals via `countMacroSubgraphIssues`. Becomes BLOCKING from STEP 3.
3. **Ports/rows**: `BehaviourStepNode` `hiddenPorts` hides Radius/Area (Body) · Bond Degree (Bonds) · Age (Lifespan); the Properties [AgentCapabilitiesSection.tsx](src/modeler/panels/AgentCapabilitiesSection.tsx) preset picker + toggles + live footprint; SimulatorView's `agentEditPanelAttrs` + the inspector popover filter geometry rows to enabled capabilities.

### STEP 0 — shared layout-agnostic ABI descriptor ([src/modeler/vpl/compiler/agentAbi.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/agentAbi.ts))
The three CPU-side agent-ABI mirrors (compile.ts `buildAgentLoopParams`/`buildDivisionParams`/`buildAgentInitParams` NAME strings, the worker's `buildAgentLoopArgs`/`buildDivisionArgs`/`buildAgentInitArgs` VALUE arrays, the parity harness `buildArgs`) now ALL derive from ONE source — `deriveAgentAbi(kind, shape[, profile])`. Each field bundles its param NAME + a `get(store, rt)` value resolver, so name and value are defined together and the four sites **cannot desync in order** (the "3D dimsModel/total" desync class). The descriptor takes a lightweight `AgentAbiShape` (primitives — `is3d`/`agentAttrs`/`fieldAttrs`/`hasLookupTables`) so the worker (which keeps no model) derives it from its own state (`agentAbiShapeOfStore` + `agentAbiBaseRt`). `buildAgentAbiParams` (names) + `buildAgentAbiArgs` (values, used by BOTH worker + harness). An optional per-field `gate(profile)` is wired but a **no-op in STEP 0** — the descriptor is currently profile-unaware, so the output is byte-identical to the pre-refactor mirrors (this is the prerequisite that makes STEP 3+ field gating a one-place change). `is3dModelLike` (niCodec) — NOT `is3dModel` (compile.ts) — avoids a circular import.

### Verification (every SoA-touching step must re-pass these)
- `node scripts/parity-agent-wasm.mjs` — JS↔WASM 0 mismatches on all 9 agent samples (the harness now CONSUMES the shared `buildAgentAbiArgs`, so it verifies the loop-args path the worker uses).
- `node scripts/test-agent-abi.mjs` — proves `buildAgentAbiArgs` for loop/division/init is element-for-element identical to the old hand-written builders (2D/3D × ±lookup × ±hash); covers division+init, which parity never runs.
- `node scripts/audit-agent-layout.mjs` — the 4-site field-order audit (compile params === descriptor per kind; 2D a strict prefix of 3D; no dup names; valid cTypes). The cross-target SoA field-SET arm (CPU `AGENT_*_FIELDS` vs WebGPU `AGENT_GPU_*_FIELDS`) activates in STEP 3 when the layout becomes profile-gated.
- `node scripts/test-agent-capabilities.mjs` — 67 asserts (preset stability, Full≡Morphogenesis, migration never hides a used node on any shipped sample, gating, removal cascades, footprint ordering).
- Browser-verified: the Properties panel renders (Boids 141 B, Social Graph 125 B); Growing Tissue seeds (init) + grows 12→1500 agents over 203 steps (division + loop) with 0 errors — so all three descriptor-derived ABI paths run at runtime.

### STEP 3 — first real SoA gate: Bonds (SHIPPED)
The Bonds capability is AUTHORITATIVE for the ragged bond store. `resolveMaxBonds` ([centerBased.ts](src/model/centerBased.ts)) is profile-aware — `bonds === 'off'` ⇒ **0** regardless of the config's `maxBonds` ceiling, riding the already-tested maxBonds=0 code path (pure-force models). So a Boids/Chemotaxis/Ant model that never forms bonds no longer pays `28·maxBonds·maxAgents` for a store it never touches (~112 KB at 2000 agents), while a bonded tissue keeps its store. **Byte-identical output** for any bond-free model (the compiled per-agent code loops `b < bondCount[idx]` = 0 and never indexes the 0-length store). The GPU layout's maxBonds now also goes through `resolveMaxBonds` (was a direct read — the desync fix); the auto-bond scan is gated on `s.maxBonds > 0`; the load-state stride check (M1) rejects a mismatch only when the saved state HAS bonds. `scripts/test-bonds-allocation.mjs` asserts it. Verified: parity 0 mismatches; Boids on WebGPU (resolvedTarget=webgpu, effMaxBonds=0) flocks 0.999. Field coupling needs no SoA change (agentAccess already prunes the `_field_` signature). **The badge stays INFORMATIONAL** (a placed capability-off node still compiles as a no-op — making it blocking would error a leftover node that would work if the capability were re-enabled).

### Physics-capability engine wiring (Collision / Bonds=Physics / Growth — SHIPPED) — the honest-controls pass
The v1 milestone was "editor-surface only", but that left several EXPOSED capability MODES driving nothing in the engine — a **false choice** (the user-reported bug: a pure gas with **Collision = positional** but "Use bonding physics" OFF had its agents pass through each other, because the soft-sphere repulsion was gated on the coarse legacy `usesBondingPhysics` bundle). The fix DECOUPLES that bundle into per-capability engine resolvers in [centerBased.ts](src/model/centerBased.ts), each with a **legacy fallback so a profileless config is byte-identical**:
- **`usesEngineCollision(cfg)`** = `agentCapabilities ? collision !== 'off' : usesBondingPhysics(cfg)` → the soft-sphere **REPULSION** (volume exclusion). So Collision runs independently of bonding — a gas collides without cohesion/springs.
- **`usesEngineSprings(cfg)`** = `agentCapabilities ? bonds === 'physics' : usesBondingPhysics(cfg)` → the bond **SPRINGS** + the auto-bond scan. Makes the Bonds dropdown's **Data-vs-Physics a REAL distinction** (Data bonds are force-free connectivity edges; only Physics bonds spring). Previously Data and Physics unlocked the same nodes AND ran the same springs → a pure false choice.
- **`usesEngineGrowth(cfg)`** = `agentCapabilities ? growth : usesBondingPhysics(cfg)` → the growth **RAMP** (radius → targetRadius). So ticking Growth + placing Set Target Radius actually ramps (was a silent no-op without the legacy toggle).
- **Adhesion** (the free-agent cohesion `muA`) stays on `usesBondingPhysics` — it has no capability control (a numeric knob, not an exposed MODE).
- **All three targets:** JS `runAgentStep` gates springs on `springs`, growthRate on `usesEngineGrowth`; the WASM force pass adds a **`doSprings`** param (index 24, after `doCollision` at 23) gating the spring block — adhesion keeps `P.bonding`; growth rides the worker's `growthRate` arg (0 when the Growth capability is off, no bytecode change). The **WebGPU force pass has NO springs** (bonds are CPU-only on WebGPU — nothing to gate; it only got `doCollision`, correctly). `doCollision`/`doSprings` on WASM + `ForceControl.doCollision` on WebGPU were verified for ABI/slot consistency by adversarial review.
- **Byte-identity preservation:** `inferAgentProfile` widens `bonds→physics` (when `bonds !== 'off'` under `usesBondingPhysics`) and `growth→true` (when `growthRate > 0` under `usesBondingPhysics`), so a MIGRATED legacy bonding file reproduces its old spring/growth behaviour exactly (the resolvers read the SEEDED inferred profile after LOAD_MODEL). Verified (adversarial review): all 8 shipped agent models resolve collision/springs/growth to the SAME value the old `bonding` gate produced — or differ only where inert (no bonds → 0-length store → springs no-op; `growthRate === 0` → ramp no-op). The ONE non-byte-identical case (intended, matches no shipped model): a hand-constructed legacy file with the engine OFF + `growthRate > 0` + a Set Target Radius node used to freeze the ramp (a dead node); it now ramps — the previously-silent node starts working. Runtime: Growing Tissue still grows 12→768 with ~7260 bonds + the radius ramp; JS↔WASM force-pass parity ([scripts/parity-agent-force.mjs](scripts/parity-agent-force.mjs), 4 gate combos, 0 mismatches) + collision separates an overlapping blob 13×; WebGPU real-device shader compile (0 errors, 2D+3D) + end-to-end GPU dispatch (minPairDist 0.047→0.619 collision-on vs 0.047 off, matching JS/WASM exactly).

**`positional` collision — a REAL second model (see "Positional (hard) collision" below).** The honest-controls pass initially FOLDED `positional`→`soft` (it was a phantom — no distinct algorithm existed) and offered only Off / Volume exclusion. That fold was later REMOVED when positional was implemented for real: `computeCapabilityClosure` no longer collapses it, the dropdown restores **Off / Soft-sphere / Positional**, and `usesSoftCollision`/`usesPositionalCollision` gate the two distinct engine paths.

**Hidden dead rows (honest-core: never expose a toggle that unlocks nothing):** the Properties panel filters `HIDDEN_CAP_ROWS_V1` = { `populationBirth` (gates no dedicated node — the unified Create Agent / Add Agent To World spawning is always available in BOTH the Init Event and the Behaviour graph, ungated) }. **`orientation` is now UN-hidden** — it gates the FOV **`facing` heading source** (see "FOV facing heading source" below). Rows/schema/presets stay intact (a preset that sets them still matches; the footprint counts them); only the render is filtered — remove a key when its feature lands. Trimmed the Sensing description's FOV over-promise + rewrote the Motion segmented-control labels as the palette gate they are (the engine always integrates velocity in v1; a Static/Velocity integrator is the deferred STEP 6).

### Positional (hard) collision — a genuine second collision model (SHIPPED, all 3 targets, 2D+3D)
`Collision = positional` is a RIGID, no-overlap position-projection constraint — the distinct alternative to `soft` (the springy repulsion FORCE). Plan + illustrated mockup: [docs/PLAN_POSITIONAL_COLLISION.md](docs/PLAN_POSITIONAL_COLLISION.md)/`.html`.
- **Two real models now:** `soft` = a penalty force `μ_R·(d−s)` (transient overlap, non-penetrating only in the stiff limit — needs Motion=Force); `positional` = after integration, overlapping pairs are projected apart to EXACTLY touching (zero overlap, billiard-ball — works under any Motion since it edits positions). Adhesion/springs/growth orthogonal.
- **Algorithm** — `resolvePositionalCollisions` in [agentEngine.ts](src/simulator/engine/agentEngine.ts) (next to `buildSpatialHash`): a **Jacobi** position projection. Each of `positionalIterations` (config, default 2, live-tunable) sweeps reads the sweep-START positions, sums a **half-overlap push** `0.5·(s_ij−d)·(x_i−x_j)/d` per overlapping neighbour (torus-shortest, hash stencil + all-pairs), and applies all corrections at once → **order-INDEPENDENT** (identical across targets). Reuses the dead `forceX/Y[/Z]` buffers as the accumulator (**NO new per-agent SoA**); inlined interaction (V8-optimal, mirrors the force pass); the hash is rebuilt ONCE per step (sub-contact corrections rarely cross bins). A single sweep resolves an isolated pair exactly; dense packing converges over a few (the iteration knob).
- **CPU post-step on ALL targets** — the projection is target-independent CPU/JS, EXACTLY like the agent **structural phase** (bonds/division). ONE implementation, no WASM export + no GPU shader: called in `runAgentStep` between `swapPositions` and the structural phase (JS + WASM run the SAME fn over the wasmBacked memory VIEWS ⇒ **bit-identical**), and in `runAgentStepWebGPU` after `readbackAgentStep` (which already commits GPU positions to the CPU store — no extra readback; the next `uploadAgentSoA` returns the non-overlapping positions to the GPU). WebGPU's f32 force pass ⇒ f32-precision read-back positions ⇒ statistical parity (the documented WebGPU-agent stance, no worse than the CPU structural phase).
- **Gate change:** the soft-sphere repulsion moved `usesEngineCollision` → **`usesSoftCollision`** (`collision==='soft'`) at both force-pass sites, so a positional model runs the projection INSTEAD of the soft force. `collisionMode`/`usesSoftCollision`/`usesPositionalCollision` in [centerBased.ts](src/model/centerBased.ts) are the single source (legacy fallback `usesBondingPhysics ? 'soft' : 'off'`, never `positional`).
- **Byte-identical for shipped/legacy** — the inference only ever produces `soft`/`off` (never `positional`), and `usesSoftCollision` == the old `usesEngineCollision` for those. Only a hand-set `collision:'positional'` file changes (it used to fold to the soft force; now it runs the hard constraint — the intended fix). Verified: [scripts/test-positional-collision.mjs](scripts/test-positional-collision.mjs) (2D+3D torus/bounded resolve 0.01→1.0 exactly; wasmBacked bit-parity 0 mismatches; 0-iter no-op) + browser JS (off leaves overlap 0.7 vs positional→contact 2.0) + real-GPU WebGPU (routes to the webgpu target, 1.577→2.0, 0 errors). UI: the Collision dropdown restores Off / Soft-sphere / Positional + a Positional Iterations field (`AgentCapabilitiesSection`).

### STEP 5 — Get Age + Get Agents In View + Sense Hemifield (SHIPPED); Spawn / Orientation remaining
**Get Age** ([GetAgeNode.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/GetAgeNode.ts)) is the Lifespan capability's palette node — reads `_agentAge[idx]` (a byte-clone of Get Radius; JS + WASM + WebGPU emitters, `NEVER_INVARIANT` + `NEVER_PURE`).

**Get Agents In View** (STEP 5b — [GetAgentsInViewNode.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/GetAgentsInViewNode.ts)) is the directional-FOV node — the array of nearby agents inside a heading-relative **vision cone** (the Sensing capability's second node; it un-hid the Sensing FOV description). **Zero new SoA/ABI:** the cone test rides the SAME 3×3(×3) spatial-hash gather as Get Nearby Agents (`emitNearbyFill` on WASM + WebGPU was PARAMETERIZED with an optional cone, NOT cloned — getNearbyAgents keeps `cone=null` ⇒ byte-identical). Membership = `dot(heading, offset) ≥ cosHalf·|heading|·|offset|` (the division-free rearrangement of `cosA ≥ cosHalf`); `cosHalf = cos(halfAngle·π/180)` is a **compile-time literal** (`viewCosHalf`, no runtime `cos` ⇒ JS↔WASM bit-parity, no host-cos import); `|heading|²==0` (a still agent under the velocity source) ⇒ **omnidirectional** (the ÷0 guard); `halfAngle ≥ 180°` ⇒ an omni compile-time fast-path. Config: `halfAngle` (deg) + `headingSource` (**velocity** default / **wired** X/Y/Z — a `facing` source lands with Orientation, deferred to avoid an unbacked option). 2D + 3D (`hiddenPorts` hides the Z heading in 2D). **The 5 registration sites the parity harness caught** (the "half-wired feature" lesson): AGENT_WASM/WEBGPU_SUPPORTED_TYPES, AGENT_ARRAY_PRODUCERS (×both), the array-dispatch case, **AGENT_VALUE_NO_HOIST** (else the pre-emit tried to hoist it as a value → "unsupported value node"), and the **nearby scratch-slot budget** (else `slot·maxAgents·4` = `undefined·… = NaN` → `i32Const(NaN)` "leb128s: non-finite NaN"). Verified: JS 0-mismatch vs an independent cone calc (66% cull); JS↔WASM bit-parity ([scripts/parity-agent-wasm.mjs](scripts/parity-agent-wasm.mjs) `buildFOVModel` synthetic, 30 steps); WebGPU real-GPU shader compile (0 errors) + end-to-end (0 mismatch, 65% cull); Boids (getNearbyAgents on WebGPU) unregressed (polarization 0.998).

**Sense Hemifield** (STEP 5b — [SenseHemifieldNode.ts](src/modeler/vpl/nodes/SenseHemifieldNode.ts)) is the Braitenberg **LEFT / RIGHT** sensor — a MULTI-OUTPUT value node (`Left Count` + `Right Count`) that runs the SAME FOV-cone gather as Get Agents In View but, instead of an id array, SPLITS each in-view neighbour by the sign of the heading-relative **cross product** (steer by `Left − Right`). NOT an array producer → a plain multi-output value node with **`getAgentOffset`'s exact registration footprint** (MULTI_OUTPUT_TYPES + NEVER_INVARIANT + the accessorCSE never-pure list + the asyncWriteHazard reader-seed/read-keys + AGENT_WASM/WEBGPU_SUPPORTED_TYPES + a value-emitter case on each compiler) — NO scratch slot, NOT in AGENT_ARRAY_PRODUCERS, NOT in AGENT_VALUE_NO_HOIST (hoistable like getAgentOffset). Reuses `viewCosHalf`/`viewHeadingExprs` from Get Agents In View so the cone math is IDENTICAL, and the WASM/WebGPU emitters reuse the SHARED `emitHashStencil`/`emitAllPairs` (no gather drift). The split: **2D** `cross = hx·dy − hy·dx`; **3D** `cross = det[heading, offset, up]` = `select(hx·dy − hy·dx, hz·dx − hx·dz, upY)` with `up = +Z` normally, swapped to `+Y` when the heading is near-vertical (`upY = hz² > 0.81·hm2`, computed once per agent — det[h,o,+Z] reduces to the 2D form). `cross ≥ 0` ⇒ LEFT (a deterministic tie toward Left); a zero heading ⇒ omnidirectional cone ⇒ all Left. Config `halfAngle` (default **90°** = a 180° front hemisphere) + `headingSource` (velocity/wired). Verified: **tsc**; **JS logic** ([scripts/test-hemifield-logic.mjs](scripts/test-hemifield-logic.mjs)) — 5 hand-verified + independent-re-implementation cases (2D omni / 2D cone-60 / heading-rotation / 3D up-swap / 3D no-swap), all L/R exact; **JS↔WASM bit-parity** ([scripts/parity-agent-wasm.mjs](scripts/parity-agent-wasm.mjs) `buildHemifieldModel` synthetic storing BOTH outputs so a L/R swap is caught, + all 8 samples unregressed); **WebGPU real-GPU** — 2D (no `select`) + 3D (`select` with the verified operand order) shaders compile with 0 device errors.

Catalogue → **118 selectable / 45 agent**.

### Remaining — DEFERRED possible memory/compute improvements (NEED a greenlight before implementing)
**These are OPTIMIZATIONS, not missing capabilities.** Every agent capability WORKS today via the always-allocated SoA path — STEP 4/6 only shrink per-agent memory / add a cheaper integrator branch. They were deliberately NOT auto-implemented: each adds real regression surface across **all three targets'** SoA layout / force pass for a **modest, bounded reward**, so the risk/reward favours an explicit decision + a focused verification pass over an autonomous grind. The STEP 0 `field.gate` hook ([agentAbi.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/agentAbi.ts)) is already in place so STEP 4 is a one-place change when greenlit.
- **STEP 4 — profile-gate the optional per-agent SoA fields.** Reorder `AGENT_*_FIELDS` to append-at-stable-slot, then gate the Sprite-block / Lifespan(`age`) / Growth(`targetRadius`) / Collision(`density`) fields via the descriptor's `field.gate(profile)`. **Reward: ~24–36 B/agent** (only when the owning capability is off) — e.g. a bonds-only social graph drops the sprite/age/targetRadius/density words. **The safety catch (why it's non-trivial):** dropping a field from the ABI requires that NO compiled code references it — a leftover wire into `myAge` under Lifespan=off would reference a dropped `_agentAge`. So it needs the compiler to emit a **default (0) for a dropped-field read on all 3 targets** (JS / WASM / WebGPU), plus re-passing the full verification suite (parity / gate / audit / real-GPU). First conversion of a previously-UNCONDITIONAL ABI block.
- **STEP 6 (Deferred XL) — Motion=Static SoA-gate + a Static integrator.** Motion=Static ALREADY behaves correctly via the existing velocity integrator (F=0, v=0 → no movement; GoL-on-agents proves it with momentum 0), so this is a **perf/semantics refinement, not a missing feature**. It needs NEW Static / velocity-only branches in each target's force-pass (JS `runAgentStep` + WASM `emitForcePass` + WebGPU `forcePass.ts`) so a static/pinned model skips force integration entirely, plus SoA-gating the velocity/force words when Motion=Static. XL because no Static path exists today (every target integrates velocity unconditionally in v1).
- **Recommendation:** skip both unless the memory win is specifically wanted (large agent counts where 24–36 B/agent matters). If greenlit, do STEP 4 first (smaller, the hook exists) with the default-0-emit safety catch, verify on all 3 targets, then STEP 6.
### FOV facing heading source (STEP 5 Orientation — SHIPPED, all 3 targets)
The two FOV nodes (**Get Agents In View** + **Sense Hemifield**) gained a **`facing`** heading source (alongside `velocity`/`wired`) that steers the vision cone by a stored **VECTOR agent attribute** (a direction) instead of the agent's velocity — the AutomatosGT-style oriented sensor, but trig-free (the vector IS the heading). **Zero per-target emit:** a shared pre-compile transform **`lowerFacingSource`** ([facingSource.ts](src/modeler/vpl/compiler/facingSource.ts)) rewrites a `headingSource:'facing'` FOV node into the ALREADY-VERIFIED wired composition — `getCellAttribute`(the chosen vector facing attr) → `breakVector` → the node's Heading X/Y[/Z] inputs, with `headingSource` set to `'wired'` — BEFORE `lowerVectorAttrs` (which lowers the vector read to per-component scalar reads) + `expandComposites` (which collapses the Make/Break round-trip). So the heading is the facing attribute's components on JS / WASM / WebGPU, 2D + 3D, riding the verified vector lowering + wired-cone logic. Wired into all 3 agent front-ends (JS `compileAgentGraph`, WASM/WebGPU `flattenAgentGraph`) right before `lowerVectorAttrs`; the transform clones the FOV node (never mutates the shared model node) and is a hot-path no-op unless a facing FOV node with a resolvable vector attr is present. UI: the Heading dropdown gains **Facing (vector attr)** + a vector-agent-attribute picker (CaNode). Validation: `detectMissingConfig` badges a facing node without a valid vector attr, and requires the **Orientation** capability (now un-hidden in `HIDDEN_CAP_ROWS_V1`, giving the row a real purpose). **Verified**: JS emits the facing components (`__hx=<facing_vx>`, not `_agentVX`); WebGPU reads `agentF32[<facing_vx base>+idx]` (not the `vx` offset), shader 0 errors; WASM gate+compile clean; **real-worker JS behavioural proof** — center/front/back agents all facing +x with a 60° cone give the directional pattern `seen=[1,0,1]` (each sees only agents ahead), vs the `[2,1,1]` a velocity/omni source would give. tsc + build + JS↔WASM parity (11 samples) + WASM gate + capability tests (67) all green. Follow-on: a FLOAT compass-degrees facing (sin/cos) is not implemented — the vector facing is the user-chosen representation; Sense Hemifield's optional `AheadCount` + per-side nearest dist/id remain a documented follow-on.

- **STEP 5** — DONE (Population.Birth spawning via the unified Create Agent / Add Agent To World; the FOV `facing` heading source + Orientation-row un-hide — see above). Nothing left beyond the documented follow-ons. (STEP 6 is the deferred memory/compute item — see "DEFERRED possible memory/compute improvements" above.)
